=======================================================================
45. ROUND XIV: EXACT FORCED-STRESS AND CONSTRAINED-BOUNDARY FACTORIZATION
=======================================================================

LEAN_CHECKPOINT CP45
  V16_REFINEMENT = CV02-CV06; exact symmetry actions through the actual forced inverse.
  V16_SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML24-ML25,ML50-ML52,ML59.
  V16_LOOKUP = Sections 51-55; exact source premises and target adapters retained.
  SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML07-ML25, ML26-ML33, ML34-ML42, ML49;
                     ME01-ME10, ME16, ME18-ME21, with their exact scopes.
  LOOKUP = Section 48 for additional pinned physical Fourier signatures.
  REQUIRED_BRIDGE_BODIES = FB01-FB10 below.
  PREVIOUS_BODIES = AF01-AF10, CJ01-CJ16, CG06-CG09, GE01-GE11,
    CK01-CK12, HD01-HD10, GD01-GD10.
  PARENT_PLACEMENT = NP02-NP09; Section 5 actual residual; Sections 7-11
    scalar/radial construction and its two inverse identities.
  EVIDENCE = mathematical derivation; pinned source support only;
    no matching external compiler or theorem-level axiom audit.

The original root and its canonical condition F_nu[K]=0 remain unchanged.
This round identifies the independently defined normalized vacuum ambient
sector before imposing that condition, and factors its exact correspondence
through the actual constrained boundary tensor. The forcing tensor is an
explicit additional coordinate. It is not a new quotient relation.

=======================================================================
XIV.A. THREE INDEPENDENTLY DEFINED GERM SPACES
=======================================================================

A1. Fixed parameters and the forced space.

Fix nu>0, rho!=0 and t0. All fields are actual jointly real-analytic,
periodic fields on an open cylinder about {t0} x T^3; equality is equality
after restriction to a common full spatial cylinder. Norms, signs,
unit-period Fourier normalization and marking are exactly Section 0.

Let F^omega_(nu,t0) be the pressure-gauge germs of triples (u,p,F) with

  F_ij=F_ji, tr_x F=0,
  div u=0,
  R_nu(u,p)=div F.                                  (FB.A1)

The only gauge relation is

  (u,p,F) ~ (u',p',F')
    <=> u'=u, F'=F, p'=p+a(t)
        on a common cylinder, with a analytic.        (FB.A2)

No zero-mean condition is imposed on u or F. The mean of u is necessarily
constant in time: integrate (FB.A1) over T^3, using periodicity and
div u=0 to integrate its convection term as a divergence. Thus

  partial_t <u>=0.                                   (FB.A3)

The name F^omega denotes this forced-solution space; an individual F
continues to denote its actual spatial STF stress tensor.

A2. Constrained boundary tensors, without an NSE predicate.

For any actual symmetric analytic boundary tensor K_ab define

  kappa=tr_eta K,
  B[K]_ab=kappa eta_ab-K_ab,
  beta=-tr_x K_sp,
  w_i=K_0i.

Let K^omega_(rho,t0) be the boundary-tensor germs satisfying exactly

  partial_a B[K]^{ab}=0 for b=0,1,2,3,
  H(B[K])=0,
  beta(t0,x)=rho,
  beta_t(t0,x)=0 for every x in T^3.                  (FB.A4)

Here H(B)=B_ab B^{ab}-(tr_eta B)^2/3, as before. These conditions are
given directly on K and its actual derivatives; the space is not defined
as an image of a scalar completion or a vacuum extension. It contains
no NSE solution or prescribed-force predicate. It has no dependence on nu.

A3. Normalized vacuum ambient sector.

Let Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0) be the marked analytic metric germs

  g=dr^2+h_ab(r,t,x)dy^a dy^b,
  h(0,t,x)=eta,
  K_ab=(1/2)partial_r h_ab|r=0,

with exactly one negative metric direction and satisfying

  Ric(g)=0,
  beta(t0,x)=rho,
  beta_t(t0,x)=0.                                    (FB.A5)

These are precisely the original ambient metric conditions and G1-G3.
No constitutive condition G4 has been imposed. The definition is independent
of nu. The original canonical sector is the explicit subset

  G^omega_(nu,rho,t0)
    ={g in Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0): F_nu[K_g]=0}.        (FB.A6)

The boundary restriction and radial completion below prove the first
independent equivalence

  K^omega_(rho,t0) ~= Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).           (FB.A7)

=======================================================================
XIV.B. ACTUAL FORCED CONSERVATION AND SCALAR HAMILTONIAN COMPLETION
=======================================================================

B1. Forced source tensor and the exact conservation iff.

For any analytic triple (u,p,F) with F spatial STF, not initially assuming
its field equation, define the symmetric contravariant inclusion

  iota(F)^{00}=0, iota(F)^{0i}=iota(F)^{i0}=0,
  iota(F)^{ij}=F_ij,

and set

  S_F:=S_rho[u,p]-iota(F),
  A_ij=u_i u_j+p delta_ij-nu Z(u)_ij.

Its entries are

  S_F^{00}=rho, S_F^{0i}=u_i, S_F^{ij}=A_ij-F_ij.

Direct differentiation gives

  partial_a S_F^{a0}=div u,
  partial_a S_F^{ai}
    =R_nu(u,p)_i+u_i div u-nu partial_i div u-(div F)_i.

Therefore, with no additional compatibility premise,

  partial_a S_F^{ab}=0 for every b
    <=> div u=0 and R_nu(u,p)=div F.                 (FB.B1)

Trace-freeness of F implies

  tr_eta S_F=tr_eta S_rho,
  H(S_F)=H(S_rho)-2A:F+|F|_F^2.                      (FB.B2)

Indeed, the spacetime contraction of S_rho with iota(F) is A:F; the
spacetime norm of iota(F) is the positive spatial Frobenius square;
and the trace correction is unchanged. Since tr F=0, the term A:F is
also (u tensor u-nu Z(u)):F, with no pressure contribution.

B2. Exact forced scalar normal equation.

Put B=S_F+C[psi], with the same flat divergence-free scalar correction
C^{ab}[psi]=partial^a partial^b psi-eta^{ab}Box_eta psi. The general
polarization calculation in Section 7 applies to every symmetric S_F:

  H(S_F+C[psi])
   =H(S_F)+2S_F^{ab}psi_ab
          +psi_ab psi^{ab}-(Box_eta psi)^2.

Writing Q=Hess_x psi, b=grad_x psi_t and d=tr Q, this is

  H(S_F+C[psi])
   =2(rho+d)psi_tt+H(S_F)+4u.b+2(A-F):Q
                  -2|b|^2+|Q|_F^2-d^2.              (FB.B3)

The equation H(B)=0 is therefore exactly

  psi_tt = Fsc_F(t,x,Q,b)
   :=[-H(S_F)-4u.b-2(A-F):Q+2|b|^2-|Q|_F^2+d^2]
                         /[2(rho+d)].                (FB.B4)

All occurrences of u,p,F and derivatives of u in this expression are
actual prescribed analytic source fields. The additional F coordinate
enters the numerator polynomially. It introduces no psi_tt term and
does not alter the normal coefficient. At the prescribed data

  psi(t0,x)=0, psi_t(t0,x)=0,

one has Q=0, b=0 and

  partial H(S_F+C[psi])/partial psi_tt=2rho!=0.       (FB.B5)

Thus Fsc_F is analytic on the open reduced-jet neighborhood where
rho+d!=0. Its active psi derivative slots are exactly the same six
symmetric Q entries and three b entries as for the unforced normal map.
The generic second-normal-order CK formulation still has 14 reduced
scalar jet coordinates. No increase in unknown dimension is needed.

B3. Actual scalar completion and domains.

Apply the constructively derived CK12 theorem to (FB.B4) at each initial
torus point. The analytic finite source coefficients, nonzero denominator
and zero Cauchy traces verify its actual hypotheses. GD01-GD04 apply
to the periodic translated coefficients: local uniqueness gives
vertical overlap equality, a finite spatial cover gives a common positive
time interval, and continuity/compactness give the margin

  |rho+Delta psi|>=|rho|/2>0                          (FB.B6)

on a smaller full cylinder, by the same compact-continuity argument used
in GD05. Thus there exists a unique actual normalized
analytic periodic scalar germ satisfying H(S_F+C[psi])=0.

This assertion holds even before (FB.B1) is assumed. The constraint
conservation follows only when the triple obeys (FB.A1). There is no
smallness condition on the analytic compatible stress, pressure or
velocity. The data-dependent CK radii and coefficient bounds remain
those of the actual forced normal map; no uniform Sobolev radius follows.

=======================================================================
XIV.C. COMPLETED BOUNDARY DATA AND INTRINSIC STRESS RECOVERY
=======================================================================

C1. Trace reversal and all constraint identities.

Assume now the actual forced equations (FB.A1). Set

  B=S_F+C[psi],
  K_ab=(tr_eta B)eta_ab/3-B_ab.                       (FB.C1)

Equations (FB.B1) and div C=0 give div B=0; B3 gives H(B)=0.
Trace reversal gives

  kappa=(tr_eta B)/3,
  B_ab=kappa eta_ab-K_ab,
  K_ab K^{ab}-kappa^2=H(B)=0.                         (FB.C2)

Since tr_eta iota(F)=0, the exact boundary formula is

  K=K^0[u,p;rho]+iota(F)_lower-Hess_(t,x)psi.         (FB.C3)

For this divergence-free input its components are

  K_00=-(2rho+|u|^2)/3-p-psi_tt,
  K_0i=u_i-psi_ti,
  K_ij=((|u|^2-rho)/3)delta_ij-u_i u_j
                         +nu Z(u)_ij+F_ij-psi_ij.    (FB.C4)

Taking the spatial trace and using div u=tr F=0 yields

  beta=rho+Delta psi,
  w=u-grad psi_t,
  q=G(beta-rho)=psi-<psi>,
  v=P w=u.                                          (FB.C5)

The zero scalar traces give beta(t0)=rho and beta_t(t0)=0. Hence K
belongs to the independently defined space K^omega_(rho,t0).

C2. Exact constitutive stress and pressure recovery.

Use the original K-only definitions

  F_nu[K]=TF(K_sp+v tensor v-nu Z(v)+Hess_x q),
  p_*=-K_00-(2rho+|v|^2)/3-q_tt.

Substituting (FB.C4)-(FB.C5), using Hess_x q=Hess_x psi and tr F=0,
gives the actual identities

  F_nu[K]=F,
  p_*=p+partial_t^2<psi>.                            (FB.C6)

The temporal mean derivative is analytic by the already-derived analytic
Fourier/mean interface. Therefore reconstruction returns exactly the same
forced triple pressure-gauge germ. No stress projection or stress gauge
is hidden in this identity: the entire tensor F is recovered.

C3. Initial forced boundary jet, without a pressure choice.

At t=t0, the spatial derivatives of both zero scalar traces vanish. Put

  A0_F:=((|u0|^2-rho)/3)I-u0 tensor u0+nu Z(u0)+F0.

Then

  K_0i(t0)=u0_i,
  K_sp(t0)=A0_F,
  tr A0_F=-rho,
  K_00(t0)=(|A0_F|_F^2-2|u0|^2-rho^2)/(2rho).       (FB.C7)

For the final identity write kappa=-K_00-rho at t0 in (FB.C2):

  0=K_00^2-2|u0|^2+|A0_F|_F^2-(K_00+rho)^2.

Solving this linear equation for K_00 gives (FB.C7), since rho!=0.
The initial scalar normal derivative is also explicitly

  psi_tt(t0)=-H(S_F)(t0)/(2rho).                      (FB.C8)

Its pressure dependence cancels exactly in (FB.C4), as required by the
time-pressure gauge. Setting F0=0 recovers the original initial-jet
formula. Formula (FB.C7) does not assert that arbitrary initial u0,F0
extend to a compatible analytic forced triple; that existence premise
is supplied by the full input germ or by the explicit construction of
Round XV, within its exact hypotheses.

=======================================================================
XIV.D. CONSTRAINED BOUNDARY DATA ARE EXACTLY THE NORMALIZED VACUUM GERMS
=======================================================================

D1. Boundary restriction.

For g in Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0), the actual signed Gaussian geometry
derived in GE01-GE11 gives at r=0

  partial_a B[K_g]^{ab}=0,
  H(B[K_g])=0.

The two remaining conditions of (FB.A4) are exactly the normalization
conditions already assumed for g. Equality of marked metric germs gives
equality of their boundary normal derivatives on a common full time
cylinder, by GD07. Hence

  J:Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0)->K^omega_(rho,t0),
  J([g])=[(1/2)h_r|r=0]                              (FB.D1)

is an actual well-defined boundary restriction map.

D2. Radial extension from arbitrary constrained K.

Given K in K^omega_(rho,t0), prescribe h|0=eta and h_r|0=2K in the
actual radial normal equation Ric(g)_ab=0. The input is analytic and
symmetric; det eta=-1. CK12 therefore gives the actual local symmetric
analytic solution. GD01-GD04 and GD06 give a common product collar,
preserving Lorentzian signature and invertibility. Its initial Hamiltonian
and Codazzi constraints are exactly (FB.A4). GE11's actual contracted
Bianchi propagation consequently gives Ric(g)=0 on that collar.

Thus the radial construction defines

  E:K^omega_(rho,t0)->Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).           (FB.D2)

Its germ is independent of all local construction choices and of the K
representative: equal boundary data give equal actual radial solution
germs by CK normal uniqueness and GD vertical continuation.

D3. The two radial inverse laws.

The prescribed initial derivative immediately gives J(E([K]))=[K].
For any existing g in the normalized vacuum ambient sector, both g and
E(J(g)) solve the same radial normal equation and have the same h|0=eta,
h_r|0=2K_g. Actual radial analytic uniqueness on a common product collar
gives E(J([g]))=[g]. Therefore (FB.A7) is an exact bijection, with

  J o E=id, E o J=id.                                (FB.D3)

No NSE existence theorem and no constitutive equation appear in this
boundary/vacuum equivalence. The boundary space was specified independently
by actual constraints. The equality remains equality of marked metric
arrays after restriction; it is not equality up to a bulk diffeomorphism.

=======================================================================
XIV.E. BOUNDARY RECONSTRUCTION AND EXACT COMPLETION FIBRES
=======================================================================

E1. Reconstruction directly from a constrained K.

Let K satisfy (FB.A4). None of the following steps needs a preselected
vacuum extension. The 0-Codazzi component gives beta_t+div w=0 and
partial_t<beta>=0. The normalization yields <beta>=rho. Define

  q=G(beta-rho), v=P w=w+grad q_t,
  p_*=-K_00-(2rho+|v|^2)/3-q_tt,
  F=F_nu[K].

Then q(t0)=q_t(t0)=0, div v=0, and the actual tensor identity is

  B[K]=S_rho[v,p_*]-iota(F)+C[q].                    (FB.E1)

Proof. The 00 and 0i components follow from Delta q=beta-rho and the
displayed v identity. The spatial trace defines p_* as in Section 4.
The spatial trace-free component is exactly minus the defining F_nu[K],
as in (5.1). Thus all components agree. Conservation of B[K] and C[q]
combined with (FB.B1) gives

  div v=0, R_nu(v,p_*)=div F.                         (FB.E2)

All operators preserve actual joint analyticity on a common smaller
cylinder by AF and GD07. Consequently

  D_nu:K^omega_(rho,t0)->F^omega_(nu,t0),
  D_nu([K])=[v,p_*,F_nu[K]]                          (FB.E3)

is well defined. Here nu chooses the stress coordinate; it does not
alter the constrained K space or the boundary/vacuum equivalence.

E2. Exact pressure-gauge cancellation.

For a gauge a(t), let f=-I2a with f''=-a and f(t0)=f'(t0)=0. Holding
u,F fixed, one has

  S_F[u,p+a,F]-S_F[u,p,F]=a(t)iota(I),
  C[psi+f]-C[psi]=f''(t)iota(I),

where iota(I) here denotes only the spatial identity inclusion, without
a trace-free condition. Therefore

  S_F[u,p+a,F]+C[psi+f]=S_F[u,p,F]+C[psi].            (FB.E4)

The shifted scalar has the prescribed zero data and solves the shifted
Hamiltonian equation; scalar uniqueness identifies it with that normalized
completion. Thus K and its radial vacuum germ are unchanged. This proves
well-definedness on (FB.A2), using the actual normalized double primitive
already constructed in CG06 and not a pressure witness choice.

E3. Complete fibres of the forced completion tensor.

Suppose two analytic triples and normalized scalar potentials satisfy

  S_rho[u',p']-iota(F')+C[psi']
     =S_rho[u,p]-iota(F)+C[psi],                      (FB.E5)

with both F,F' STF and both scalar Cauchy traces zero. No forced PDE
assumption is needed for this fibre calculation. Their 00 components
give Delta(psi'-psi)=0. Periodic harmonic uniqueness yields
psi'-psi=f(t). Their 0i components give u'=u. Their spatial components
then give

  F'-F=(p'-p+f'')I.

Taking the spatial trace and using trace-freeness shows p'-p=-f'' and
then F'=F. Let a=-f''. The zero scalar traces imply f=-I2a. Conversely
these relations imply (FB.E5) by (FB.E4). Hence

  equal completed B
    <=> exists analytic a(t):
          u'=u, F'=F, p'=p+a, psi'=psi-I2a.           (FB.E6)

Trace reversal is an exact invertible linear map, so the same statement
holds for equal completed K. For radial solutions with h|0=eta and
h_r|0=2K, equality of K is also equivalent to equality of the actual
marked metric germs, by D3. This last metric implication requires the
displayed radial equation and Cauchy data; it is not a statement about
arbitrary off-shell metrics sharing their first boundary jet.

=======================================================================
XIV.F. BOTH FORCED INVERSE LAWS, CANONICAL RESTRICTION AND EXACT IFF
=======================================================================

F1. Forced triple to boundary equivalence.

Let C_(nu,rho):F^omega_(nu,t0)->K^omega_(rho,t0) be the scalar completion
and trace reversal from B3-C1, descending by E2 and restriction uniqueness.
Equations (FB.C5)-(FB.C6) give

  D_nu o C_(nu,rho)=id.                              (FB.F1)

Conversely, for K in the boundary space, (FB.E1) and H(B[K])=0 show
that q solves exactly the forced scalar equation for (v,p_*,F_nu[K]),
with zero Cauchy traces. Its initial coefficient is 2rho. Scalar analytic
uniqueness on a common cylinder gives q=psi for that reconstructed triple.
Trace reversal of (FB.E1) then recovers the original K exactly. Hence

  C_(nu,rho) o D_nu=id.                              (FB.F2)

This proves the independent first equivalence in the factorization

  F^omega_(nu,t0) ~= K^omega_(rho,t0)
                         ~= Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).   (FB.F3)

F2. Exact forced bulk correspondence.

Define the explicit composite maps

  L_forced=E o C_(nu,rho),
  R_forced=D_nu o J.

Equations (FB.D3), (FB.F1) and (FB.F2) give, with no additional premise,

  R_forced o L_forced=id,
  L_forced o R_forced=id.                            (FB.F4)

Every normalized analytic marked vacuum germ is therefore represented
by a unique forced triple modulo only its analytic time-pressure gauge.
Every compatible analytic forced triple has a unique such vacuum germ.
The bulk statement supplies no independent source of force regularity:
the full analytic compatibility equation (FB.A1) is part of its domain.

F3. The original root is exactly the zero-stress restriction.

The map [u,p] |-> [u,p,0] identifies N^omega_(nu,t0) with the subset
{[u,p,F] in F^omega_(nu,t0): F=0}. The latter condition is well defined
because the gauge fixes the full F. By the exact recovery (FB.C6),

  F=0 <=> F_nu[K]=0

under both boundary and bulk correspondences. Thus (FB.F3)-(FB.F4)
restrict to exactly the original

  N^omega_(nu,t0) ~= G^omega_(nu,rho,t0).             (FB.F5)

Both restricted maps equal the original scalar/radial lift and boundary
reconstruction, by their actual formulas and uniqueness. The canonical
sector is not replaced by projected-forcing zero or full-divergence zero.

F4. Quantified lift iff for an arbitrary analytic STF triple.

For any analytic periodic u,p,F with F symmetric trace-free, the following
are equivalent as local germs:

  (i) div u=0 and R_nu(u,p)=div F;

  (ii) there exist an analytic periodic psi with psi|t0=psi_t|t0=0
       and a marked analytic Lorentzian vacuum g with h|r=0=eta and
       boundary tensor

         K=(tr_eta S_F)eta/3-(S_F)_lower-Hess_(t,x)psi.
                                                               (FB.F6)

The forward implication is the construction above. For the reverse,
trace reversal gives B[K]=S_F+C[psi]; actual vacuum Codazzi gives
div B[K]=0; flat derivative commutation gives div C[psi]=0. Hence
div S_F=0, and the exact iff (FB.B1) proves (i).

Vacuum Hamiltonian also gives H(S_F+C[psi])=0. With the prescribed zero
data and rho!=0, scalar uniqueness fixes psi; radial uniqueness fixes
the metric germ. Therefore the witness pair in (ii) is unique as a pair
of normalized scalar and marked metric germs for the given representative.

The trace formula in (FB.F6) is used for arbitrary u; the simplified
component formula (FB.C4) is invoked only after div u=0 has been proved.
This avoids inserting the divergence-free trace identity into the reverse
direction before it is available.

=======================================================================
XIV.G. ATOMIC CONTRACTS AND SOURCE PLACEMENT
=======================================================================

FB01 FORCED_CONSERVATION_IFF
  Input: actual analytic periodic u,p and symmetric trace-free F.
  Output: (FB.B1)-(FB.B2), including the exact sign of the stress inclusion.
  Proof: B1; actual component derivatives and finite Lorentzian contraction.
  Support: ML07-ML19; existing NP03-NP04; no PDE existence endpoint used.

FB02 FORCED_SCALAR_NORMAL_MAP
  Input: the same fields, rho!=0 and the exact reduced scalar jet.
  Output: (FB.B3)-(FB.B5), analytic Fsc_F on rho+tr Q!=0, unchanged
    noncharacteristic coefficient and 14-coordinate reduced CK interface.
  Proof: B2; finite polarization and the actual rational expression.
  Support: ML13-ML16, ML26-ML27; CJ01-CJ03 are adapted with A-F and H(S_F).

FB03 ACTUAL_NORMALIZED_FORCED_SCALAR_COMPLETION
  Input: FB02's actual analytic coefficient map and zero Cauchy traces.
  Output: unique actual periodic analytic scalar germ and margin (FB.B6).
  Proof: B3; CK12 and GD01-GD04 with the displayed forced coefficients,
    followed by the explicit compact-continuity scalar margin argument.
  Support: ML20-ML21, ML29-ML30, ML36, ML40-ML42; no missing CK premise.

FB04 FORCED_BOUNDARY_CONSTRAINTS_AND_RECOVERY
  Input: an actual forced triple and FB03's actual scalar completion.
  Output: (FB.C1)-(FB.C8), K in the independently defined constrained
    boundary space, exact full-stress recovery, and initial boundary jet.
  Proof: C1-C3; FB01-FB03, actual trace reversal and analytic means.
  Support: ML07-ML19; AF07-AF10; no stress projection replaces F.

FB05 INDEPENDENT_BOUNDARY_VACUUM_EQUIVALENCE
  Input: actual spaces defined separately by (FB.A4) and (FB.A5).
  Output: well-defined J,E and both exact inverse laws (FB.D3).
  Proof: D1-D3; actual GE constraints, CK12, GD06 and radial uniqueness.
  Support: ML20-ML25, ML31-ML33, ML49; geometry supplied by GE01-GE11.

FB06 FORCED_RECONSTRUCTION_FROM_CONSTRAINED_K
  Input: K satisfying exactly (FB.A4), with nu>0.
  Output: (FB.E1)-(FB.E3), actual analytic forced triple and reconstruction
    independent of its full-cylinder representative.
  Proof: E1; Codazzi mean identity, actual Poisson/Leray and trace/TF split.
  Support: AF01-AF10, ML01-ML12, ME01-ME10, ME16, ME18-ME21, GD07.

FB07 EXACT_FORCED_COMPLETION_FIBRES
  Input: analytic triples, STF stresses and normalized scalar potentials.
  Output: the full iff (FB.E6), pressure-gauge invariance, and the stated
    equality consequences for completed K and actual radial solutions.
  Proof: E2-E3; harmonic uniqueness, component equality and zero-data primitive.
  Support: CG06-CG09, ML34-ML39, ME06, GD07-GD09; no additional stress gauge.

FB08 FORCED_TRIPLE_BOUNDARY_TWO_INVERSES
  Input: FB03-FB04, FB06-FB07 and the independently specified domains.
  Output: (FB.F1)-(FB.F2), hence F^omega_(nu,t0) ~= K^omega_(rho,t0).
  Proof: F1; exact recovered full stress, pressure gauge and scalar uniqueness.
  Support: ML22-ML25, ML49 after all representative invariance is proved.

FB09 FORCED_BULK_EQUIVALENCE_AND_CANONICAL_RESTRICTION
  Input: FB05, FB08 and the explicit maps E,C,D,J.
  Output: (FB.F3)-(FB.F5), preserving the original F_nu[K]=0 root exactly.
  Proof: F2-F3; composition of the actual inverse laws and full-stress recovery.
  Support: ML24-ML25; packaging alone proves no analytic or geometric premise.

FB10 ARBITRARY_TRIPLE_EXACT_VACUUM_LIFT_IFF
  Input: an arbitrary analytic periodic u,p,F with F spatial STF, nu>0,rho!=0.
  Output: exact quantified iff (FB.F6), with unique normalized witnesses.
  Proof: F4; vacuum Codazzi forces the actual forced PDE, then scalar
    Hamiltonian and radial uniqueness force the two constructed germs.
  Support: FB01-FB09; original geometry/CK bodies; no divergence-free
    simplification used before the reverse implication establishes it.

DEPENDENCY_SPINE:
  actual source algebra -> FB01 -> FB02 -> FB03 -> FB04;
  actual boundary constraints + radial CK/geometry -> FB05;
  actual boundary reconstruction -> FB06;
  normalized primitive + exact component fibres -> FB07;
  FB03-FB04 + FB06-FB07 -> FB08;
  FB05 + FB08 -> FB09;
  FB01 + FB03-FB09 + actual vacuum Codazzi -> FB10.

MATCHING_EXTERNAL_COMPILATION_AND_AXIOM_AUDIT = ABSENT.
