46. ROUND XV: COMPLETE VACUUM FIBRES AND VISCOSITY-INDEPENDENT COORDINATES
=======================================================================

LEAN_CHECKPOINT CP46
  V16_REFINEMENT = SF08,CV04,VI04-VI06; actual witnesses and classified viscosity fibres.
  V16_SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML55-ML62; ME23-ME24.
  V16_LOOKUP = Sections 51-55; exact source premises and target adapters retained.
  SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML01-ML16, ML24-ML29, ML49;
                     ME01-ME10, ME16-ME22.
  LOOKUP = Section 48 for actual physical Fourier and viscosity signatures.
  REQUIRED_BRIDGE_BODIES = VF01-VF10 below.
  DEPENDENCIES = FB01-FB10; AF01-AF11; CG01-CG05; HD01-HD10;
                 GD01-GD10; FC07-FC10 where finite jets are compared.
  EXACT_ROLE = Parameterize the entire normalized analytic vacuum sector,
    then recover the original canonical sector as its exact zero-stress slice.
  EVIDENCE = complete mathematical derivations; source applications uncompiled.

Fix rho!=0 and t0. Let Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0) denote the normalized marked
analytic vacuum germs satisfying G1-G3 of Section 1, without G4. This
ambient sector has no viscosity parameter in its definition. Its members
remain five-dimensional Lorentzian vacuum metrics in the fixed Gaussian
marking; no diffeomorphism quotient is introduced.

For nu>0, let F^omega_(nu,t0) denote the forced triple space of Section 45:
jointly analytic periodic triples (u,p,F), with F real spatial STF,
div u=0 and R_nu(u,p)=div F, modulo p->p+a(t), with u and F unchanged.
Section 45 proves the actual equivalence F^omega_(nu,t0) ~= Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).

In this section G is the mean-zero inverse Laplacian, extended by zero
on the constant mode. To distinguish the scalar stress operator from the
spacetime completion tensor C_eta[psi], write

  A(f):=Z G f,
  C_x(phi):=(3/2) TF Hess_x G phi.                    (VF.1)

For mean-zero solenoidal f and mean-zero phi, the exact identities are
div A(f)=f and div C_x(phi)=grad phi. Their real analytic versions and
all zero-mode conventions were proved in Section 39.

=======================================================================
XV.A. VELOCITY PATHS AND A COMPLETE FORCED NORMAL FORM
=======================================================================

A1. Independent coordinate spaces.

Define the following real vector spaces of germs at the full time slice:

  U^omega_(t0): jointly analytic periodic vector fields u with
                 div u=0 and partial_t<u>=0;
  M^omega_(t0): analytic time germs with values in STF_3(R);
  Phi^omega_(t0): jointly analytic periodic scalar germs phi with <phi>=0;
  TT^omega_(t0): jointly analytic periodic STF tensor germs T with
                 <T>=0 and div T=0.

The velocity mean in U^omega is an arbitrary constant vector, not zero.
Equality means actual equality on a smaller common full time cylinder.
Finite products of these germ spaces have representatives on a common
cylinder by taking the positive minimum of their four domain radii.
GD07-GD08 prove all restriction and quotient identities used below.

For u in U^omega and nu>0 set

  f_nu(u):=u_t-nu Delta u+P div(u tensor u),
  pi(u):=-G div div(u tensor u).                     (VF.2)

Every term is jointly analytic on a possibly smaller common cylinder,
by the actual Fourier/derivative maps AF01-AF11. Derivative commutation
and div u=0 imply div f_nu(u)=0. Periodicity gives zero spatial means
for Delta u and div(u tensor u), and P retains the zero mode. Hence

  <f_nu(u)>=partial_t<u>=0,
  <pi(u)>=0,
  R_nu(u,pi(u))=f_nu(u).                             (VF.3)

The last equality is the actual Helmholtz residual identity CG.1 with F=0.

Conversely, for any analytic forced triple, integration of
R_nu(u,p)=div F over the normalized torus gives partial_t<u>=0: the
convective term is div(u tensor u), while all divergences, gradients and
Laplacians integrate to zero. Thus every forced triple's velocity belongs
to U^omega. The constant-mean condition is both necessary and sufficient
for the forced-stress construction below; it is not an NSE evolution law.

A2. Construction from arbitrary independent coordinates.

For (u,M,phi,T) in the displayed product, put

  F_nu[u,M,phi,T]
    :=M+A(f_nu(u))+C_x(phi)+T,
  p[u,phi]:=pi(u)+phi.                               (VF.4)

All tensors are real, jointly analytic, periodic and STF. M is spatially
constant. Equations (VF.1)-(VF.3) give

  div F_nu=f_nu(u)+grad phi=R_nu(u,p[u,phi]).          (VF.5)

Thus (VF.4) is an actual forced triple. There is no amplitude-smallness
condition on M,phi,T or u. The completion theorem supplies a data-dependent
local collar; it does not supply one radius uniform over these coordinates.

A3. Necessity and exact inverse formulas.

Let (u,p,F) be an actual analytic forced triple. Applying P to its equation
and using div u=0 gives

  P div F=f_nu(u).                                   (VF.6)

Taking divergence and using the periodic Poisson inverse gives

  p-<p>=pi(u)+G div div F.                           (VF.7)

Define

  M=<F>, phi=G div div F, T=Pi_T F.                  (VF.8)

HD05 and HD10 prove that these are members of their respective coordinate
spaces and that

  F=M+A(f_nu(u))+C_x(phi)+T.

The original pressure differs from pi(u)+phi by exactly the allowed
analytic time gauge <p>. Hence (VF.4) and (VF.8) are inverse maps on the
exact forced triple quotient:

  F^omega_(nu,t0)
    ~= U^omega_(t0) x M^omega_(t0) x Phi^omega_(t0) x TT^omega_(t0).
                                                               (VF.9)

No stress equivalence is taken: distinct M,phi,T at a fixed u represent
distinct forced triple classes, even if their full equation residuals agree.
Uniqueness follows from the four actual orthogonal projections, not merely
from equality of divergence.

=======================================================================
XV.B. ACTUAL VACUUM PARAMETRIZATION AND ITS INVERSE
=======================================================================

B1. The complete normalized vacuum chart.

Compose (VF.4) with the actual forced lift of Section 45. This gives

  Xi_(rho,t0):
    U^omega_(t0) x M^omega_(t0) x Phi^omega_(t0) x TT^omega_(t0)
       -> Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).                     (VF.10)

For the moment the construction may use any fixed nu>0. Its independence
of that choice is proved below. The actual inverse, computed from K, is

  u=P K_(0 bullet),
  M=<F_nu[K]>,
  phi=G div div F_nu[K],
  T=Pi_T F_nu[K].                                    (VF.11)

For an input quadruple, the forced bridge reconstructs u and F_nu exactly,
and reconstructs p up to its time gauge. HD05 therefore recovers M,phi,T
exactly. For an input metric, the forced bridge reconstructs a forced triple;
(VF.6)-(VF.8) identify that triple with (VF.4), and the inverse forced lift
returns the original marked metric germ. Therefore both inverse identities
hold and

  Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0)
    ~= U^omega_(t0) x M^omega_(t0) x Phi^omega_(t0) x TT^omega_(t0).
                                                               (VF.12)

This is an equivalence of the explicitly defined germ sets. No topology,
Banach-manifold structure, linear metric operation or norm continuity of
the nonlinear lift is inferred from the product coordinates alone.

B2. Viscosity cancels from the conserved completion source.

For every solenoidal analytic u,

  G Delta u=u-<u>,
  Z G Delta u=Z u.                                  (VF.13)

The first equality is coefficientwise, including the zero mode; the second
uses the spatial constancy of <u>. Define f_0(u)=u_t+P div(u tensor u).
Although the root is stated for nu>0, this auxiliary expression merely
names an analytic vector field. Then

  f_nu(u)=f_0(u)-nu Delta u,
  A(f_nu(u))=A(f_0(u))-nu Z u.                       (VF.14)

The spacetime source used in the forced scalar completion is

  Sigma_rho:=S_(rho,nu)[u,pi(u)+phi]-iota(F_nu[u,M,phi,T]).

Its components reduce to

  Sigma^(00)=rho,
  Sigma^(0i)=u_i,
  Sigma^(ij)=u_i u_j+(pi(u)+phi)delta_ij
               -M_ij-A(f_0(u))_ij-C_x(phi)_ij-T_ij.  (VF.15)

This expression contains no nu. It is conserved directly:
the 0 component is div u=0, while the spatial components are
u_t+P div(u tensor u)-f_0(u)=0, using (VF.1) and the pressure identity.

Consequently the exact scalar Hamiltonian completion equation, its two
normalized Cauchy traces and the resulting K are identical for all nu>0.
Scalar uniqueness identifies the completions on a common cylinder; radial
uniqueness identifies their marked vacuum metric germs. Thus Xi in (VF.10)
is genuinely independent of viscosity. Viscosity enters the canonical
admission condition inside these coordinates, not the ambient vacuum chart.

=======================================================================
XV.C. EXACT FIBRES, ADMISSION LEVELS AND REALIZED STRICT INCLUSIONS
=======================================================================

C1. Fibres of the recovered velocity.

Let V(g)=P K_(0 bullet). For every u in U^omega, (VF.12) restricts to

  V^(-1)({u})
    ~= M^omega_(t0) x Phi^omega_(t0) x TT^omega_(t0). (VF.16)

Surjectivity of V follows by choosing M=phi=T=0. This proves an actual
vacuum realization for every jointly analytic periodic solenoidal velocity
history with constant mean. It does not assert that the history solves NSE.
The compatible forcing and its exact pressure are the expressions (VF.4).

C2. The canonical root is the exact zero-fibre restriction.

For a metric with coordinates (u,M,phi,T), orthogonal decomposition gives

  F_nu[K]=0
    <=> f_nu(u)=0 and M=0 and phi=0 and T=0.           (VF.17)

By CG.5, f_nu(u)=0 is exactly NSE_nu(u,pi(u)). Thus

  G^omega_(nu,rho,t0)
    =Xi_(rho,t0)(V^omega_(nu,t0) x {0} x {0} x {0}).  (VF.18)

The original N^omega ~= V^omega ~= G^omega equivalence is precisely this
restriction of (VF.12). No root premise or canonical tensor condition changes.

Define two intermediate subsets of Gtilde:

  G_samep(nu):={g: R_nu(V(g),p_*(g))=0},
  G_proj(nu):={g: exists analytic periodic p, NSE_nu(V(g),p)}.

The geometric p_* is the original K-only pressure reconstruction, which
is unchanged by viscosity. The exact coordinate descriptions are

  G_samep(nu) <=> f_nu(u)=0 and phi=0,
  G_proj(nu)  <=> f_nu(u)=0.                          (VF.19)

M and T are free in both subsets; phi is also free in G_proj. These are
equalities of actual predicates, from R_nu(u,p_*)=div F_nu and (VF.5).
The normalized reconstructed pressure is always pi(u)+phi.

C3. All three strict inclusions have actual vacuum witnesses.

For every nu>0,rho!=0,t0,

  G^omega_(nu,rho,t0)
    proper_subset G_samep(nu)
    proper_subset G_proj(nu)
    proper_subset Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).              (VF.20)

First inclusion. Take u=0, phi=T=0 and the nonzero constant STF matrix
M=diag(1,-1,0). Then f_nu=0,p=0,F_nu=M. Its forced lift is an actual
normalized vacuum metric by Section 45. It lies in G_samep but not in
the canonical sector because its reconstructed constitutive tensor is M.

Second inclusion. Take u=0, M=T=0, phi=cos(2*pi*x_1). Then
f_nu=0, p=phi and F_nu=C_x(phi). Explicitly,

  F_nu=cos(2*pi*x_1)*diag(1,-1/2,-1/2),
  div F_nu=grad phi!=0.

The actual lift belongs to G_proj, with corrected NSE pressure zero,
but does not belong to G_samep. Adding a time-only gauge to p_* does not
change its nonzero spatial gradient.

Third inclusion. Take the stationary analytic shear

  u(t,x)=e_1 cos(2*pi*x_2), M=phi=T=0.

Its mean and divergence vanish; div(u tensor u)=0 and pi(u)=0. Therefore

  f_nu(u)=4*pi^2*nu*u!=0,
  F_nu=-nu Z u,
  p=0.

The forcing equation holds exactly. Its actual normalized vacuum lift
does not belong to G_proj, because applying P to any proposed NSE
equation would force the nonzero vector f_nu to vanish.

These witnesses use the actual compatible-triple existence theorem.
They are stronger than the purely algebraic tensor witnesses in Section 39.
They do not claim that arbitrary unrelated F and u satisfy compatibility.
Bulk matter remains zero in every witness: the bulk equation is Ric(g)=0.

=======================================================================
XV.D. A UNIQUE MINIMUM CONSTITUTIVE-DEFECT METRIC IN EACH VELOCITY FIBRE
=======================================================================

For g=Xi(u,M,phi,T), the pointwise-in-time orthogonal norm identity is

  ||F_nu[K(g)]||_2^2
    =2||f_nu(u)||_(dot H^-1)^2
       +|M|_F^2+(3/2)||phi||_2^2+||T||_2^2.          (VF.21)

Define the actual metric germ

  g_min(u):=Xi(u,0,0,0).                             (VF.22)

It has F_nu[K(g_min)]=A(f_nu(u)), the minimum-norm STF tensor with the
given projected force. The same g_min(u) works for every nu>0 by B2.

For any other metric germ g with the same recovered velocity u, choose
a>0 so that both metric germs and all compared boundary fields have
representatives on a common neighborhood of [t0-a,t0+a] x T^3. Such an a
exists by shrinking their common open time cylinder. Define on these
representatives the finite actual boundary objective

  J_(nu,a)(g):=integral_(t0-a)^(t0+a)||F_nu[K(g)](t)||_2^2 dt.

Integrating (VF.21) gives

  J_(nu,a)(g)-J_(nu,a)(g_min(u))
    =integral_(t0-a)^(t0+a)
       (|M|_F^2+(3/2)||phi||_2^2+||T||_2^2)dt >=0.  (VF.23)

Equality holds if and only if g=g_min(u) as a metric germ. To prove the
nontrivial direction, the integrand is nonnegative and continuous. If
its integral is zero, its value is zero throughout the open interval.
Then M=0, while the zero L^2 norms and spatial continuity imply phi=T=0
pointwise on the full common cylinder. The inverse coordinate map gives
metric-germ equality. Conversely, metric-germ equality gives zero free
coordinates near t0. Analytic continuation on the common connected time
interval, applied at each spatial point, makes them zero throughout the
compared interval, so its integral vanishes.

Thus g_min is the unique germ minimizing this objective on every common
sufficiently small closed time interval. No universal time interval across
all possible germs is assumed. A minimum at one isolated time would not
identify a metric germ, and no such assertion is made. The objective is
the boundary constitutive norm, not a distance between bulk metrics or a
minimization of bulk curvature.

In particular,

  g_min(u) in G^omega_(nu,rho,t0) <=> f_nu(u)=0.       (VF.24)

For actual NSE velocity germs it is exactly the original canonical lift.
For arbitrary u in U^omega it remains a normalized vacuum realization
with its explicitly minimal compatible constitutive residual.

=======================================================================
XV.E. VISCOSITY TRANSPORT AND THE EXACT CANONICAL INTERSECTION
=======================================================================

E1. Forced-triple transport.

For nu,mu>0 define

  T_(nu,mu)[u,p,F]:=[u,p,F-(mu-nu)Z(u)].              (VF.25)

Since div u=0, tr Z(u)=0 and div Z(u)=Delta u. Hence

  R_mu(u,p)=R_nu(u,p)-(mu-nu)Delta u
           =div(F-(mu-nu)Z(u)).                     (VF.26)

The transformed stress is real analytic STF, and the map respects the
unchanged time-only pressure gauge. Direct addition proves

  T_(nu,nu)=id,
  T_(mu,lambda) o T_(nu,mu)=T_(nu,lambda),
  T_(mu,nu) o T_(nu,mu)=id.                          (VF.27)

The completed spacetime source is exactly preserved:

  S_(rho,mu)[u,p]-iota(F-(mu-nu)Z(u))
    =S_(rho,nu)[u,p]-iota(F).                        (VF.28)

Thus the scalar completion and boundary K coincide by uniqueness, and
the radial metric germs coincide. The transport acts as the identity
on Gtilde under the exact forced bridge, rather than changing the metric.

E2. Intrinsic coordinate invariance.

For the same actual K, reconstruction gives the same u,q,p_* for every
viscosity, while the definition of F gives

  F_mu[K]=F_nu[K]-(mu-nu)Z(u),
  f_mu(u)=f_nu(u)-(mu-nu)Delta u.                    (VF.29)

The tensor Z(u) lies entirely in the Pi_v range:

  <Z(u)>=0,
  G div div Z(u)=0,
  Pi_v Z(u)=Z G Delta u=Z(u),
  Pi_T Z(u)=0.                                      (VF.30)

Therefore M,phi,T in (VF.11) are invariant under viscosity relabeling;
u and the normalized pressure pi(u)+phi are invariant as well. This
also proves directly that the inverse chart is viscosity-independent.

E3. Distinct canonical viscosity sectors intersect exactly at constants.

Suppose nu!=mu and g belongs to both canonical sectors. By (VF.17), its
free coordinates M,phi,T vanish and f_nu(u)=f_mu(u)=0. Subtracting gives
Delta u=0. Each periodic harmonic component has only its zero Fourier
mode, so u(t,x)=c(t). Then f_nu(u)=c'(t)=0, hence u is one constant
vector c in R^3 on a common cylinder.

Conversely any constant vector u=c has f_nu(u)=0 for every nu>0, and
Xi(c,0,0,0) lies in every canonical viscosity sector. Therefore

  G^omega_(nu,rho,t0) intersect G^omega_(mu,rho,t0)
    ={Xi_(rho,t0)(c,0,0,0):c in R^3}, nu!=mu.        (VF.31)

The constant-velocity parameter is injective by reconstruction. This
recovers the original viscosity-rigidity theorem inside the complete
vacuum chart, without changing either sector's definition.

=======================================================================
XV.F. EXACT INITIAL BOUNDARY TRACE IMAGE AND A FINITE-JET OBSTRUCTION
=======================================================================

F1. Every admissible initial velocity/STF pair is realized in Gtilde.

Let u0(x) be any real-analytic periodic divergence-free vector field, and
let F0(x) be any real-analytic periodic STF tensor. No NSE time-analytic
existence premise is imposed on u0. Define

  u1=nu Delta u0-P div(u0 tensor u0)+P div F0,
  u(t,x)=u0(x)+(t-t0)u1(x).                          (VF.32)

All terms are analytic and periodic. Their divergences vanish and <u1>=0,
so u belongs to U^omega. Moreover f_nu(u)(t0)=P div F0. Take the free
coordinates constant in time, with

  M=<F0>, phi=G div div F0, T=Pi_T F0.               (VF.33)

The full stress decomposition gives

  F_nu[u,M,phi,T](t0,x)=F0(x).                       (VF.34)

The actual metric Xi(u,M,phi,T) therefore realizes the given velocity
and stress at the reference slice. Its initial boundary jet, using
the forced completion identity FB.C7, is

  K_(0i)(t0)=u0_i,
  K_sp(t0)=A0_nu(u0)+F0,
  A0_nu(u0):=((|u0|^2-rho)/3)I-u0 tensor u0+nu Z(u0),
  K_00(t0)=(|K_sp(t0)|_F^2-2|u0|^2-rho^2)/(2rho).   (VF.35)

The first two identities also follow from the zero scalar Cauchy traces:
the spatial and mixed Hessians of psi vanish at t0. For the last identity,
tr K_sp=-rho and the actual flat-boundary Hamiltonian equation is

  K_ab K^ab-(tr_eta K)^2
    =|K_sp|_F^2-2|u0|^2-2rho K_00-rho^2=0.

Division by 2rho is valid for either sign of rho. Pressure does not occur
in (VF.35), because its contribution is compensated by the scalar second
time derivative in the exact Hamiltonian completion.

F2. Exact intrinsic image of the initial boundary trace.

Let I^omega_rho be the set of analytic periodic symmetric 4x4 fields J(x)
satisfying

  tr_x J_sp=-rho,
  div_x J_(0 bullet)=0,
  J_ab J^ab-(tr_eta J)^2=0.                           (VF.36)

The actual map g |-> K_g(t0,.) has image exactly I^omega_rho. Necessity:
G2 fixes the spatial trace; G3 and the temporal Codazzi equation give
div K_(0 bullet)=0; vacuum gives the Hamiltonian equation. Sufficiency:
for J satisfying (VF.36), set u0=J_(0 bullet) and
F0=J_sp-A0_nu(u0). This F0 is STF because both spatial traces are -rho.
The construction (VF.32)-(VF.35) gives K0i=J0i and K_sp=J_sp. The last
equation in (VF.36), with rho!=0, determines J00 by the same rational
formula (VF.35). Thus every component agrees.

This is a surjectivity theorem for the initial boundary trace of the
larger forced ambient sector. It is not surjectivity of analytic NSE
initial traces, and it does not identify a metric germ from one such trace.

In particular every real-analytic STF tensor F0, including the real
single-frequency sharpness witnesses of HD04/HD09, occurs as the actual
F_nu[K](t0). Their sharp norm ratios are therefore also attained at the
reference slice of normalized analytic vacuum metrics. This is an
ambient-sector initial-slice conclusion; it does not claim a nonzero
constitutive tensor in the canonical F=0 sector or prescribe arbitrary
incompatible full-time u,F pairs.

For two actually realized data with the same u0 and stress difference
D=F0'-F0, the exact pointwise initial variation is

  K'_(0i)-K_(0i)=0,
  K'_sp-K_sp=D,
  K'_00-K_00=(2K_sp:D+|D|_F^2)/(2rho).               (VF.37)

Thus a spatial STF modification is accompanied by a specific nonlinear
temporal component. Arbitrarily adding only D to K_sp would generally
violate the Hamiltonian equation; (VF.37) records the required correction.

F3. No fixed finite metric jet determines canonical germ admission.

Fix any integer N>=0 and set ell=max(2,N). Choose a nonzero constant real
STF matrix M_*. Consider the coordinates

  u=0, phi=T=0, M(t)=(t-t0)^ell M_*.                   (VF.38)

Their actual vacuum metric g_ell=Xi(0,M,0,0) has F_nu[K(g_ell)]=M(t),
which is nonzero as an analytic germ. Hence g_ell is not canonical for
any nu>0. Let g_c=Xi(0,0,0,0), which is canonical for every nu>0.

The completed boundary tensors here are explicitly

  K_(0i)=0,
  K_sp=-(rho/3)I+M(t),
  K_00=-rho/3+|M(t)|_F^2/(2rho).                    (VF.39)

For verification, the data are spatially translation-invariant. Uniqueness
of scalar completion therefore makes psi time-only, so q=psi-<psi>=0.
The constitutive and reconstructed-velocity identities give the first two
formulas. The Hamiltonian equation gives the third, since tr M=0.
Equivalently, (VF.39) directly satisfies the Hamiltonian equation, has
beta=rho and w=0, and its trace reversal is conserved: its 00 component
is the constant rho, its 0i components vanish, and its spatial divergence
vanishes. The exact boundary-to-vacuum equivalence therefore applies.

Compared with M=0, the difference of K_sp vanishes to time order ell,
the difference of K_00 vanishes to order 2ell, and there is no spatial
dependence. Thus all tangential K jets of total order<=ell-1 coincide
at every (t0,x). The radial equation has the same analytic normal map,
initial h=eta, and h_r=2K. The exact finite-jet dependency theorem FC07
therefore gives

  j^ell_(r,t,x) g_ell(0,t0,x)=j^ell_(r,t,x) g_c(0,t0,x)
       for every x in T^3.                           (VF.40)

In particular their jets through order N coincide along the entire
initial compact slice, while one germ satisfies G4 and the other does not.
Consequently no predicate depending only on that fixed order-N metric
jet can equal canonical germ admission on all Gtilde members.

This concerns jet-only admission tests. It does not exclude certificates
that contain additional global analytic identities, all-order recurrence
proofs or other independently checked data. FC05/FC08 still certify their
exact finite jets and remainder estimates; they never asserted G4 from
finite coefficient vanishing alone.

=======================================================================
XV.G. ATOMIC CONTRACTS AND EXACT SOURCE PLACEMENT
=======================================================================

VF01 VELOCITY_HISTORY_MEAN_AND_PROJECTED_FORCE
  INPUT: an analytic forced triple, or u in the independently defined U^omega.
  OUTPUT: necessity of constant mean; analytic f_nu,pi and (VF.2)-(VF.3).
  PROOF: XV.A1; periodic divergence integration, actual P/G and derivative maps.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML01-ML12; ME01-ME10,ME16-ME22; AF01-AF11,CG01.

VF02 COMPLETE_FORCED_TRIPLE_NORMAL_FORM
  INPUT: coordinates (u,M,phi,T), or an actual forced triple modulo p+a(t).
  OUTPUT: (VF.4)-(VF.9), including both exact inverse maps and analytic domains.
  PROOF: XV.A2-A3; HD05/HD10 and canonical pressure identity.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML24-ML29,ML49; ME06,ME16,ME18,ME20; GD07-GD08.

VF03 ACTUAL_NORMALIZED_VACUUM_FIBRE_EQUIVALENCE
  INPUT: VF02 and the actual forced equivalence FB01-FB10.
  OUTPUT: (VF.10)-(VF.12) and velocity fibres (VF.16), actual marked germs.
  PROOF: XV.B1 and XV.C1; the displayed two inverse computations.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML24-ML25,ML49 only package already-proved inverse bodies;
    all actual CK, geometry, nonlocal operators and germ descent remain FB/GD.

VF04 VISCOSITY_INDEPENDENT_CONSERVED_SOURCE
  INPUT: the exact coordinate construction, div u=0 and constant mean.
  OUTPUT: (VF.13)-(VF.15), source conservation, nu-independent actual lift.
  PROOF: XV.B2; zero-mode-aware G Delta identity and exact viscous cancellation.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ME01-ME05,ME20-ME22; ML11-ML16; scalar/radial uniqueness FB/GD.

VF05 CANONICAL_ZERO_FIBRE_AND_ADMISSION_COORDINATES
  INPUT: an actual normalized vacuum metric and its VF03 coordinates.
  OUTPUT: (VF.17)-(VF.19), the unchanged canonical root as the exact restriction.
  PROOF: XV.C2; orthogonal STF decomposition and actual reconstructed residual.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ME06,ME16; CG01-CG05,HD08; actual pressure gauge retained.

VF06 REALIZED_STRICT_ADMISSION_INCLUSIONS
  INPUT: nu>0,rho!=0,t0 and the three explicit compatible coordinate choices.
  OUTPUT: all strict inclusions (VF.20) with actual analytic vacuum witnesses.
  PROOF: XV.C3; explicit Fourier modes and FB actual completion/lift.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML01-ML06; ME01-ME05,ME06; no abstract tensor witness is
    silently promoted to a metric witness without the proved FB construction.

VF07 UNIQUE_MINIMUM_CONSTITUTIVE_DEFECT_METRIC_FIBRE
  INPUT: fixed u in U^omega, actual fibre members and a common closed time interval.
  OUTPUT: (VF.21)-(VF.24), existence and metric-germ uniqueness of g_min.
  PROOF: XV.D; HD orthogonality, actual realization, positive continuous integral.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML03,ML05,ML38-ML39 where integrating; HD03-HD06 and GD07.
  DOMAIN: comparison on any sufficiently small common interval; no universal
    interval or bulk metric-distance assertion is supplied.

VF08 EXACT_VISCOSITY_TRANSPORT_COCYCLE
  INPUT: actual forced triples, nu,mu,lambda>0, fixed rho,t0.
  OUTPUT: (VF.25)-(VF.30), cocycle/inverses, unchanged actual metric and
    invariant intrinsic u,M,phi,T and pressure coordinates.
  PROOF: XV.E1-E2; exact div Z identity, source cancellation and uniqueness.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ME01-ME05,ME20-ME22; ML11-ML16; HD01-HD05; FB/GD.

VF09 DISTINCT_VISCOSITY_CANONICAL_INTERSECTION
  INPUT: nu!=mu, both positive, and the two original canonical sectors.
  OUTPUT: exact set equality (VF.31), with injective constant-velocity parameter.
  PROOF: XV.E3; subtract projected equations, periodic harmonic uniqueness,
    constant-time derivative and the exact vacuum chart.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML01-ML06; ME06; original Section 21 and CG05/HD08.

VF10 EXACT_INITIAL_TRACE_IMAGE_AND_FINITE_JET_NONADMISSION
  INPUT: analytic solenoidal u0 and STF F0, or intrinsic J satisfying (VF.36);
    for the finite-jet separation, any N>=0 and a nonzero constant STF M_*.
  OUTPUT: actual realization (VF.32)-(VF.35), exact initial image (VF.36),
    nonlinear temporal correction (VF.37), and genuine vacuum witnesses
    (VF.38)-(VF.40) with equal finite jets but unequal canonical admission.
  PROOF: XV.F1-F3; HD decomposition, FB.C7, actual constraint/radial uniqueness,
    and FC07's finite normal-jet dependency with ell=max(2,N).
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML01-ML16,ML17-ML19; ME01-ME06,ME16,ME20-ME22; FB/GD/FC bodies.
  SCOPE: normalized forced ambient sector only; no time-analytic NSE solution
    from arbitrary analytic u0, and no impossibility result for richer checkers.

DEPENDENCY_SPINE:
  actual P/G and periodic calculus -> VF01;
  VF01 + HD05/HD10 + CG01 -> VF02;
  VF02 + FB exact forced bridge -> VF03;
  exact G Delta identity + scalar/radial uniqueness -> VF04;
  VF03 + HD orthogonality -> VF05 -> VF06;
  VF03 + HD norm identity + positive time integral -> VF07;
  VF04 + actual Z/div algebra -> VF08 -> VF09;
  VF01-VF03 + FB.C7 + FC07 -> VF10.

ROOT_THEOREM = UNCHANGED_ZERO_STRESS_RESTRICTION.
NEW_AMBIENT_FIBRE_THEOREM = MATHEMATICALLY_DERIVED.
ACTUAL_COMPATIBLE_VACUUM_REALIZATION = DERIVED_VIA_FB.
UNRESTRICTED_NSE_INITIAL_TRACE_EXISTENCE = NOT_ASSERTED.
LOCAL_LEAN_EXECUTION = NONE.
MATCHING_EXTERNAL_COMPILATION_AND_AXIOM_AUDIT = ABSENT.
A_TIER = NOT_ESTABLISHED.

=======================================================================
47. ROUND XVI: ANALYTIC PARAMETERS AND EXACT LINEARIZED EQUIVALENCE
=======================================================================

LEAN_CHECKPOINT CP47
  SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML07-ML16, ML26-ML33, ML41-ML42, ML50-ML54;
                     ME06, ME18, ME20-ME22, with their exact hypotheses.
  REQUIRED_BRIDGE_BODIES = PL01-PL10 below.
  PREVIOUS_BODIES = AF01-AF10, CG01-CG09, CJ01-CJ16, GE01-GE11,
    CK01-CK12, GD01-GD10 and the actual forced correspondence FB01-FB10.
  LOOKUP = Section 48 for the new exact pinned derivative/source signatures.
  EVIDENCE = mathematical proof; target derivatives and equivalences uncompiled.

All family assertions below concern a specified finite-dimensional real
parameter and actual jointly analytic representatives on a common smaller
domain. No topology or Frechet manifold structure on the whole set of
analytic germs is assumed. The original nonlinear root is unchanged.

=======================================================================
XVI.A. PARAMETER-DEPENDENT CK WITHOUT AN ADDITIONAL EXISTENCE ENGINE
=======================================================================

A1. Parameter extension of the exact second-order normal theorem.

Let lambda range in an open neighborhood of lambda0 in R^p, p>=1.
Suppose the normal map F(lambda,s,y,J^-_2 U), and the Cauchy data
a(lambda,y), b(lambda,y), are jointly real analytic, with the initial
reduced jet in the same declared open coefficient domain. Then there
exists an actual solution U(lambda,s,y), jointly analytic in all these
variables near (lambda0,0,y0), satisfying

  U_ss=F(lambda,s,y,J^-_2 U),
  U(lambda,0,y)=a(lambda,y),
  U_s(lambda,0,y)=b(lambda,y).                         (PL.A1)

Proof. Treat (y,lambda-lambda0) as d+p tangential coordinates. Extend F
to the corresponding reduced-jet space by projection onto the original
U,U_s,U_y,U_yy,U_sy slots; ignore every derivative slot involving a
lambda index. This projection is finite-dimensional and linear, so the
extended map is analytic. The original initial-jet condition is exactly
the pulled-back open-domain condition. CK12 for d+p tangential variables
therefore proves (PL.A1). Its actual equation contains no parameter
derivatives, so every fixed-lambda slice satisfies the original equation.
Normal jet uniqueness proves joint local uniqueness. QED.

The proof changes the tangential indexing, not the normal order, equations
or data. It does not assert first-time-order CK existence for NSE from
arbitrary spatially analytic initial traces.

A2. Common parameter domains for the actual bridge.

Suppose (u_lambda,p_lambda,F_lambda) is an actual jointly analytic family
of compatible forced triples on a neighborhood of

  {lambda0} x {t0} x T^3,

with a common full spatial slice. The constants nu(lambda)>0 and
rho(lambda)!=0 may themselves be analytic functions of lambda alone;
t0 is fixed. Shrink the parameter neighborhood so that these inequalities
persist. For scalar completion, apply A1 with tangential coordinates
(x,lambda). Its highest normal coefficient at the zero data is
2rho(lambda), nonzero near lambda0. A finite cover of the compact set
{lambda0} x T^3 and the product-neighborhood argument GD08 give a
parameter neighborhood O_lambda and delta>0 on which the actual psi is
jointly analytic. GD01-GD04 identify all overlaps. Compact continuity,
after a further strict restriction, bounds |rho(lambda)+Delta psi|
away from zero on a common parameter/time/spatial product.

For radial completion, the resulting K(lambda,t,x) is jointly analytic.
Choose a smaller closed time interval about t0 and cover
{lambda0} x [t0-a,t0+a] x T^3 by finitely many tangential CK charts.
Apply A1 to the radial equation, with (lambda,t,x) tangential. The same
finite product-minimum construction gives one parameter neighborhood,
one positive radial radius and one time cylinder on which h(lambda,r,t,x)
is jointly analytic. Since h(lambda,0,t,x)=eta, compact continuity gives
a common Lorentzian inverse/signature margin. Every parameter slice is
the actual normalized vacuum lift from FB, by its inverse/uniqueness
theorem. In particular, an actual family with F_lambda=0 has slices in
the original canonical sector. No new root premise is introduced.

A3. Analytic reparametrization and gauge naturality.

If b:O_mu->O_lambda is analytic with b(mu0)=lambda0, then

  psi_[data o b](mu,t,x)=psi_[data](b(mu),t,x),
  h_[data o b](mu,r,t,x)=h_[data](b(mu),r,t,x)         (PL.A2)

on a common smaller product domain. The right sides are analytic, satisfy
the pulled-back actual equations and the same zero/flat Cauchy data,
and hence agree with the left sides by scalar and radial uniqueness.
The reparametrization depends only on the auxiliary parameters; it
therefore does not alter physical t,x or the marked normal r.

For an analytic time-gauge a(lambda,t), the normalized double primitive
I2a is jointly analytic. This follows either from its convergent joint
coefficient series or by differentiating its explicit bounded Volterra
integral on a smaller parameter/time product. The compensation

  p -> p+a(lambda,t), psi -> psi-I2a(lambda,t)

preserves B,K and the actual metric family. All choices of local charts,
majorants, radii and representatives are removed by the same uniqueness
arguments. Thus (PL.A2) is an identity of the original family germs,
including their exact pressure gauge and fixed marking.

A4. Differentiation through the actual Fourier operators.

Joint analyticity near the compact spatial slice gives a common smaller
complex parameter/time domain and spatial strip by AF01. Repeating the
finite-parameter Cauchy estimates in AF09 supplies exponential Fourier
bounds for each fixed finite parameter derivative. Absolute convergence
then permits differentiation under each Fourier sum and spatial mean.
The time-independent symbols of P and G give

  partial_lambda <u>=<partial_lambda u>,
  partial_lambda(Pu)=P(partial_lambda u),
  partial_lambda(Gu)=G(partial_lambda u).             (PL.A3)

All equalities hold on an actual common smaller full spatial product.
Mixed parameter/time/spatial derivatives commute because the functions
are jointly analytic. ML50-ML52 provide the corresponding actual-map
chain/linear derivative interfaces once these analytic space/domain
adapters have been proved. They do not supply those adapters themselves.

=======================================================================
XVI.B. THE TWO EXACT LINEARIZED SOLUTION SPACES
=======================================================================

B1. Fixed canonical base and fluid linearization.

For the rest of this section fix nu>0,rho!=0,t0 and an actual canonical
metric germ g=dr^2+h with boundary K. Use its original reconstruction

  u=P K_0bullet, p=p_*, q=G(beta-rho),
  B=B[K]=S_rho[u,p]+C[q].                            (PL.B1)

Then (u,p) is an analytic NSE solution, q has zero Cauchy data,
<q>=0, B^{00}=beta=rho+Delta q, and |beta|>=|rho|/2 on a smaller
full cylinder. This fixes a convenient actual representative of the base;
the resulting statements are invariant under the original pressure gauge.

Define the actual linearized fluid operator

  L_(u,nu)(v,pi)
    :=v_t+(u.grad)v+(v.grad)u+grad pi-nu Delta v.      (PL.B2)

Let Nlin^omega_u be the real vector space of analytic periodic pairs
(v,pi), modulo pi->pi+a(t), satisfying

  div v=0, L_(u,nu)(v,pi)=0.                         (PL.B3)

No initial condition is prescribed for v; it must already be an actual
jointly analytic solution of the displayed linearized system. Integrating
that system gives partial_t<v>=0. Its canonical zero-mean pressure is

  pi_lin(v)=-G div div(u tensor v+v tensor u).        (PL.B4)

Indeed, take the divergence of (PL.B2), use div u=div v=0, and obtain
Delta pi=-div div(u tensor v+v tensor u). Mean-zero Poisson uniqueness
proves (PL.B4) up to the stated time gauge. Hence (PL.B3) is equivalently

  div v=0,
  v_t-nu Delta v+P div(u tensor v+v tensor u)=0,       (PL.B5)

with (PL.B4) as an explicit pressure representative. Analyticity follows
from the actual AF product/Poisson and parameter-free synthesis bodies.

B2. Actual metric variation, without a bulk diffeomorphism quotient.

Let gamma_ab(r,t,x) be a symmetric analytic periodic tensor germ with
gamma_ab(0,t,x)=0. Extend it to a five-dimensional tensor by
gamma_rr=gamma_ra=0. Set

  k_ab=(1/2)partial_r gamma_ab|r=0,
  b=-tr_x k_sp,
  wlin_i=k_0i,
  qlin=G(b-<b>), vlin=P wlin.                        (PL.B6)

The actual Ricci variation is

  DRic_g[gamma]=partial_epsilon Ric(g+epsilon gamma)|epsilon=0. (PL.B7)

The expression is defined after a common local restriction: the base
metric is invertible with a Lorentzian margin, and continuity preserves
that margin for sufficiently small epsilon on a compactly restricted
collar. This definition uses ordinary finite-dimensional coefficient
derivatives of the actual coordinate Ricci expression. It is linear in
gamma and its first/second coordinate derivatives.

Define the linearized constitutive tensor

  Flin_(g,nu)[k]
    :=TF(k_sp+u tensor vlin+vlin tensor u
                     -nu Z(vlin)+Hess_x qlin).       (PL.B8)

Let Glin^omega_g be the real vector space of such tensor germs satisfying

  DRic_g[gamma]=0,
  b(t0,x)=0, b_t(t0,x)=0,
  Flin_(g,nu)[k]=0.                                 (PL.B9)

These are independently stated linear equations on an actual tensor
germ. Equality is equality on a common marked product collar. There is
no quotient by infinitesimal diffeomorphisms and no assumption that the
tensor is the derivative of a family of exact canonical metrics.

=======================================================================
XVI.C. ACTUAL GEOMETRIC VARIATION AND LINEAR CONSTRAINT PROPAGATION
=======================================================================

C1. Inverse, connection and Ricci derivatives.

Use I,J,A,B for the five coordinates. Differentiating
(g+epsilon gamma)(g+epsilon gamma)^(-1)=I gives

  delta g^{IJ}=-g^{IA}gamma_AB g^{BJ}.                (PL.C1)

Writing A^I_JK=delta Gamma^I_JK, differentiation of the actual Christoffel
formula followed by metric compatibility gives

  A^I_JK=(1/2)g^{IL}
     (nabla_J gamma_LK+nabla_K gamma_LJ-nabla_L gamma_JK). (PL.C2)

For clarity, this identity has no independent geometric premise: its raw
coordinate form is (1/2)(delta g^{IL})(partial_J g_LK+partial_K g_LJ
-partial_L g_JK)+(1/2)g^{IL}(partial_J gamma_LK+partial_K gamma_LJ
-partial_L gamma_JK). Substitute (PL.C1), expand the three covariant
derivatives and use the symmetry of Gamma; the connection terms are
exactly the first raw term.

Differentiate the Ricci convention GE.2, including its quadratic terms.
The result can be collected as

  (DRic_g[gamma])_BJ=nabla_A A^A_JB-nabla_J A^A_AB.   (PL.C3)

To check the collection, the ordinary derivatives are partial_A A^A_JB
-partial_J A^A_AB. The product derivatives are the four terms obtained
by varying one Christoffel factor in each Ricci product. Expanding the
two covariant derivatives of the (1,2)-tensor A yields exactly those
terms; the remaining lower-index corrections cancel by torsion freedom.
Thus (PL.C3) follows from the same finite index formulas as the original
metric equations, with no independent linearized-curvature axiom.

At the vacuum base, the scalar and Einstein variations are

  delta R=g^{IJ}(DRic_g[gamma])_IJ,
  delta G=DRic_g[gamma]-(1/2)(delta R)g.              (PL.C4)

Differentiate the actual Bianchi identity for g+epsilon gamma. Every
term containing a varied connection or inverse metric multiplies the
base G or its covariant derivative, both zero. Hence

  nabla^I(delta G)_IJ=0.                             (PL.C5)

This is valid for every analytic gamma as above, whether or not it solves
the linearized vacuum equation. ML53 provides the unit-inverse derivative
interface only after its actual matrix/normed-algebra adapter is supplied;
the multiplication order in (PL.C1) is fixed.

C2. Linear boundary constraints.

Since the boundary metric of g+epsilon gamma is exactly eta, its flat
Gauss--Codazzi identities can be differentiated without a boundary-metric
variation term. Put ell=tr_eta k and

  X[k]^{ab}=ell eta^{ab}-k^{ab}.

For a tensor solving DRic_g[gamma]=0, the differentiated boundary
constraints are precisely

  partial_a X[k]^{ab}=0,
  kappa*ell-K_ab k^{ab}=0.                           (PL.C6)

The second identity is one half of the variation of kappa^2-K_ab K^{ab}.
Equivalently, if DH_B denotes the derivative of H at the actual B,

  DH_B(X)=2[B_ab X^{ab}-(tr_eta B)(tr_eta X)/3],
  DH_B(X[k])=0.                                     (PL.C7)

The equivalence follows by differentiating the actual trace-reversal
identity H(B)=K_ab K^{ab}-kappa^2. Differentiating a proved algebraic
identity introduces no extra constraint assumption.

C3. Linear radial equation and zero-defect propagation.

Write V=h_r and J=h^(-1). The actual radial map is

  h_rr=2Ric[h]-kappa V+VJV, kappa=(1/2)tr(JV).

Its linearization is the analytic linear normal equation

  gamma_rr=2 DRic_h[gamma]-(delta kappa)V-kappa gamma_r
       +gamma_r J V+V J gamma_r-V J gamma J V,
  delta kappa=(1/2)tr(J gamma_r-J gamma J V).         (PL.C8)

Here DRic_h is the variation of the intrinsic four-dimensional tangential
Ricci expression at fixed r; it involves at most two tangential
derivatives of gamma. The radial equation is already solved for gamma_rr.
Its coefficients are actual analytic functions of the fixed invertible
base h and its derivatives. The generic CK12 theorem therefore gives
an analytic solution for gamma|0=0, gamma_r|0=2k for every analytic k.
The same normal recursion gives uniqueness, and GD01-GD04 provide a
common periodic marked collar. No new CK existence engine is required.

If k satisfies (PL.C6), that solution is linearized vacuum. To prove this,
differentiate the exact off-shell residual identities GE.25-GE.26 at the
vacuum base. The base residuals and their derivatives vanish, so all
coefficient-variation terms multiplying them vanish. Equation (PL.C8)
sets delta Ric_ab=0. For e=(delta G)_rr, m_a=(delta G)_ra, the remaining
identities become

  e_r=-D_a m^a-2kappa e,
  (m_a)_r=partial_a e-kappa m_a.                     (PL.C9)

The initial linear constraints give e|0=m|0=0. These are already-existing
analytic fields, so the homogeneous zero-normal-jet argument CJ11 and
analytic identity GD01 give e=m=0. From delta Ric_ab=0 and (PL.C4), one
has delta G_ab=-e h_ab; hence delta G=0. In dimension five its trace is
-(3/2)delta R, so delta R=0 and DRic_g[gamma]=0. This establishes the
complete linear radial-vacuum implication on an actual common collar.

=======================================================================
XVI.D. EXACT LINEAR SCALAR COMPLETION AND RECONSTRUCTION
=======================================================================

D1. Linearized conserved source and a simpler completion operator.

For (v,pi) in the linearized fluid space, define

  Sdot^{00}=0, Sdot^{0i}=v_i,
  Sdot^{ij}=u_i v_j+v_i u_j+pi delta_ij-nu Z(v)_ij.  (PL.D1)

Direct differentiation gives partial_a Sdot^{a0}=div v and
partial_a Sdot^{ai}=L_(u,nu)(v,pi)_i+u_i div v-nu partial_i div v,
because div u=0. Consequently

  div Sdot=0 <=> div v=0 and L_(u,nu)(v,pi)=0.        (PL.D2)

The scalar linear completion has unknown chi with chi|t0=chi_t|t0=0.
The flat polarization identity gives, for every analytic chi,

  DH_B(C[chi])=2 B^{ab}chi_ab.

Thus its exact equation is

  2 B^{ab}chi_ab+DH_B(Sdot)=0,                      (PL.D3)

or, using B^{00}=beta,

  chi_tt=-[4 sum_i B^{0i}chi_ti
             +2 sum_ij B^{ij}chi_ij+DH_B(Sdot)]/(2beta). (PL.D4)

The identity follows by substituting tr C=-3Box chi into (PL.C7): the
two terms involving (tr B)Box chi cancel. No derivative of B is present
in this nondivergence expression. Since div B=0, it also equals the
conservative form

  2 partial_a(B^{ab}partial_b chi)+DH_B(Sdot)=0.      (PL.D5)

The source and coefficients are actual analytic fields, and beta is
bounded away from zero on the chosen cylinder. CK12 applied to the
linear second-normal-order equation (PL.D4), with zero Cauchy data,
and GD01-GD04 give a unique actual analytic periodic chi germ. No
signature assumption on the tensor B is needed; the argument uses its
nonzero normal coefficient 2beta, initially 2rho.

D2. Linearized boundary lift.

Set X=Sdot+C[chi] and

  k_ab=(tr_eta X)eta_ab/3-X_ab.                       (PL.D6)

Then div X=0 and DH_B(X)=0. By trace reversal these are precisely
(PL.C6). The actual component formulas are

  k_00=-(2/3)u.v-pi-chi_tt,
  k_0i=v_i-chi_ti,
  k_ij=(2/3)(u.v)delta_ij-u_i v_j-v_i u_j
                                      +nu Z(v)_ij-chi_ij. (PL.D7)

They follow by differentiating the fixed-rho tensor K^0 or by substituting
(PL.D1) into (PL.D6), using div v=0. They imply

  b=Delta chi, qlin=chi-<chi>, vlin=v,
  b(t0)=b_t(t0)=0, Flin_(g,nu)[k]=0.                (PL.D8)

For the trace identity, the two (u.v) trace contributions cancel,
tr Z(v)=2div v=0, and only -Delta chi remains in tr k_sp.
The initial normalization follows from the zero chi traces. Substitution
of (PL.D7) into (PL.B8) leaves only a multiple of I, removed by TF.

Solve the actual linear radial equation (PL.C8) with these data. C3
proves that its solution gamma lies in the independently defined
linearized geometric space (PL.B9).

D3. Exact linearized reconstruction from independent geometric data.

Conversely let gamma satisfy (PL.B9), and define k,b,qlin,vlin by
(PL.B6). The first linear Codazzi equation in (PL.C6) is

  b_t+div wlin=0.

Integration over T^3 gives partial_t<b>=0. Since b(t0)=0,

  <b>=0, qlin=G b, qlin(t0)=qlin_t(t0)=0,
  vlin=wlin+grad qlin_t,
  div vlin=0.                                       (PL.D9)

Define the actual recovered pressure perturbation

  pilin=-k_00-(2/3)u.vlin-qlin_tt.                  (PL.D10)

Let Sdot[vlin,pilin] be (PL.D1), and let E=Flin_(g,nu)[k]. Finite
trace reversal and the definitions give the complete identity

  X[k]=Sdot[vlin,pilin]-iota(E)+C[qlin].             (PL.D11)

One may verify it componentwise: X^{00}=b=Delta qlin;
X^{0i}=wlin_i=vlin_i-partial_i qlin_t; and its spatial trace is fixed
by (PL.D10), while its trace-free part is exactly (PL.B8). These
trace and STF pieces uniquely determine the symmetric spatial block.

Taking the divergence of (PL.D11), using (PL.C6), (PL.D2) and div C=0,
gives the exact linearized residual identity

  L_(u,nu)(vlin,pilin)=div E.                        (PL.D12)

For E=0 this proves membership in the actual linearized NSE space.
This derivation uses the independent geometric equations; it does not
assume that gamma was produced by a family of canonical metrics.

=======================================================================
XVI.E. LINEARITY, PRESSURE GAUGE AND BOTH LINEARIZED INVERSES
=======================================================================

E1. Gauge and linear dependence.

Replacing pi by pi+a(t) changes Sdot only by a(t)I in its spatial block.
For f=-I2a, one has f''=-a and zero initial f,f'. Hence

  Sdot[v,pi+a]+C[chi-I2a]=Sdot[v,pi]+C[chi].         (PL.E1)

The same linear scalar uniqueness identifies the two completed X tensors;
their k and radial gamma are identical. Thus the lift descends to the
exact pressure quotient in (PL.B3). Conversely (PL.D10) supplies an
actual pressure representative from every geometric perturbation.

The coefficients B,h in the two linear normal equations are fixed by
the base. Their sources and data depend linearly on (v,pi). Sums and
real scalar multiples of scalar/radial solutions satisfy the summed or
scaled equations with the correct data. Uniqueness therefore proves
that the lift is real linear. Reconstruction is linear directly from
(PL.B6), (PL.B8), (PL.D10). Representatives, radii and gauge changes
commute with these maps by the full-slice restriction argument GD07-GD09.

E2. First inverse.

For a lifted (v,pi), (PL.D7)-(PL.D10) give

  vlin=v, pilin=pi+partial_t^2<chi>.                 (PL.E2)

The pressure difference is analytic and depends only on t, so it is
exactly an allowed linear pressure gauge. Thus reconstruction after
the linearized lift is the identity on Nlin^omega_u.

E3. Second inverse.

For an independent gamma in Glin^omega_g, (PL.D11) with E=0 and
DH_B(X[k])=0 says that qlin solves the same scalar equation (PL.D3)
with recovered (vlin,pilin), with zero Cauchy data by (PL.D9).
Scalar uniqueness gives chi=qlin. Consequently the rebuilt k is exactly
the original k. The original gamma and the rebuilt radial perturbation
satisfy (PL.C8), gamma|0=0 and gamma_r|0=2k. Analytic radial uniqueness
identifies them on a common marked collar. Therefore

  Nlin^omega_u ~=_R Glin^omega_g,                    (PL.E3)

with both displayed inverse maps and actual real linearity. The symbol
~=_R means a real vector-space isomorphism; it adds no Banach topology
or quotient by coordinate transformations.

For an actual analytic family of canonical solutions, differentiation
of its exact scalar and radial equations yields (PL.D3) and (PL.C8).
Their uniqueness proves that (PL.E3) is precisely the first variation
of the original nonlinear equivalence on that family. This conclusion
does not presuppose that every linearized solution integrates inside
the canonical sector.

=======================================================================
XVI.F. EXACT AMBIENT INTEGRATION AND THE PARAMETER-JET HIERARCHY
=======================================================================

F1. A genuine forced family realizing every linearized direction.

Let (v,pi) satisfy (PL.B3). For a real parameter epsilon set

  u_epsilon=u+epsilon v,
  p_epsilon=p+epsilon pi-(epsilon^2/3)|v|^2,
  F_epsilon=epsilon^2 TF(v tensor v).                (PL.F1)

These are actual jointly analytic fields on a common parameter/time/torus
domain. The divergence constraint holds exactly. Expanding the NSE
residual gives

  R_nu(u_epsilon,p_epsilon)
   =R_nu(u,p)+epsilon L_(u,nu)(v,pi)
       +epsilon^2[(v.grad)v-(1/3)grad|v|^2]
   =epsilon^2 div(TF(v tensor v))
   =div F_epsilon.                                  (PL.F2)

The second equality uses div v=0. Thus (PL.F1) is an exact compatible
forced family, not a formal family truncated modulo epsilon^3.
By A2 and FB it has an actual jointly analytic family of normalized
vacuum lifts g_epsilon with g_0=g as a marked germ. Its entire
constitutive tensor is exactly F_epsilon by FB.C6. Therefore

  partial_epsilon g_epsilon|0=Lift_lin([v,pi]),
  F_nu[K(g_epsilon)]=epsilon^2 TF(v tensor v).        (PL.F3)

For the first equality, differentiating the actual completions gives
the same source Sdot, zero first stress variation and the same scalar/
radial linear equations; uniqueness applies. It follows from (PL.E3)
that every gamma in Glin^omega_g is the derivative of an actual family
in Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0), with the displayed second-order canonical
defect. This is an ambient integration theorem with a fully explicit
compatible fluid/stress witness. It does not assert that g_epsilon lies
in the canonical F=0 sector for epsilon!=0, or that no different
canonical family with the same derivative can exist.

F2. Exact higher parameter-jet equations for existing families.

For any actual analytic forced family with fixed nu and parameter
epsilon, let

  u_j=partial_epsilon^j u_epsilon|0,
  p_j=partial_epsilon^j p_epsilon|0,
  F_j=partial_epsilon^j F_epsilon|0,

and suppose u_0=u,p_0=p,F_0=0. Repeated ordinary product differentiation
of the exact residual gives, for every n>=1,

  div u_n=0,
  L_(u,nu)(u_n,p_n)
     =div F_n-sum_(j=1)^(n-1) binomial(n,j)
                          (u_j.grad)u_(n-j).         (PL.F4)

The coefficients are actual derivatives, not Taylor coefficients divided
by factorials; this is why binomial(n,j) appears. Analyticity and A4
justify every mixed derivative and spatial operation on a common smaller
domain. The formula is a derived identity for an existing family and
does not assert convergence of a formally prescribed parameter tower.

For an exact canonical family, all F_n vanish. At second order it must
therefore satisfy

  L_(u,nu)(u_2,p_2)=-2(v.grad)v.                     (PL.F5)

The explicit ambient family (PL.F1) instead has

  u_2=0, p_2=-(2/3)|v|^2, F_2=2TF(v tensor v),

which verifies (PL.F4) exactly. This distinguishes the established
ambient integration from the additional higher-order conditions of
canonical integration. No solvability premise for (PL.F5), and no
arbitrary-trace NSE existence theorem, is inserted into the original root.

For optional scalar viscosity variation nu_epsilon=nu+epsilon mu,
the first linear equation contains the additional term -mu Delta u.
Its exact second-order ambient stress is
epsilon^2[TF(v tensor v)-mu Z(v)], with the same pressure correction,
provided the first linearized equation holds. Direct expansion and
div Z(v)=Delta v verify this statement. Positivity of nu_epsilon follows
after shrinking epsilon. ME21-ME22 provide the literal physical Fourier
diffusion interfaces; the fixed-parameter isomorphism (PL.E3) is unchanged.

=======================================================================
XVI.G. ATOMIC CONTRACTS AND PRECISE SOURCE ROLES
=======================================================================

PL01 PARAMETRIC_SECOND_ORDER_CK
  Input: actual analytic normal map/data with finitely many parameters
  and the exact CJ13 open initial-jet condition.
  Output: (PL.A1), joint analyticity and joint local uniqueness.
  Body: add tangential coordinates, project away unused parameter-jet
  slots, apply CK12 and actual normal uniqueness.
  Support: ML07-ML16, ML26-ML29; no extra existence endpoint.

PL02 PARAMETRIC_COMPLETION_AND_NATURALITY
  Input: an actual jointly analytic compatible forced family with
  positive nu(lambda), nonzero rho(lambda) and fixed t0.
  Output: common scalar/radial domains, actual vacuum family, (PL.A2)
  and analytic gauge naturality, plus (PL.A3).
  Body: PL01, FB, compact product gluing, analytic Fourier bounds and
  exact uniqueness. Support: ML20-ML23, ML26-ML29, ML34-ML42, ML49-ML52.

PL03 EXACT_LINEARIZED_NSE_AND_PRESSURE
  Input: fixed actual base (u,p) and analytic periodic perturbations.
  Output: (PL.B2)-(PL.B5), constant perturbation mean, pressure quotient
  and exact linearized conservation (PL.D1)-(PL.D2).
  Body: finite product rule, actual periodic integrations, P/G identities.
  Support: ML11-ML12, ML50-ML52, ML54; ME06,ME20-ME22 with AF adapters.

PL04 ACTUAL_GEOMETRIC_VARIATION_AND_CONSTRAINTS
  Input: fixed analytic vacuum g and actual analytic Gaussian variation.
  Output: (PL.C1)-(PL.C7), linear Bianchi and boundary constraints;
  for an existing linear radial solution, the subsidiary system (PL.C9)
  and its vacuum implication under zero initial constraints.
  Body: derivative of actual coordinate identities and zero residuals.
  Support: ML31-ML33, ML50-ML54; GE01-GE11, CJ11, GD01.

PL05 LINEAR_SCALAR_HAMILTONIAN_COMPLETION
  Input: a linearized NSE pair, actual B and |beta|>=|rho|/2.
  Output: the unique analytic normalized chi solving (PL.D3)-(PL.D5).
  Body: exact flat polarization, constructive CK12 and periodic gluing.
  Support: ML07-ML16, ML26-ML29, ML50-ML52; actual domain adapters.

PL06 LINEAR_BOUNDARY_AND_RADIAL_LIFT
  Input: PL05 and the fixed canonical base h.
  Output: (PL.D6)-(PL.D8), the exact linear constraints, actual unique
  radial solution (PL.C8) and membership in Glin^omega_g.
  Body: trace reversal, component identities, CK12, PL04 and GD gluing.
  Support: ML31-ML33, ML50-ML54; no new geometric axiom.

PL07 INDEPENDENT_LINEARIZED_RECONSTRUCTION
  Input: a tensor satisfying the independently defined equations (PL.B9).
  Output: (PL.D9)-(PL.D12) and an actual linearized NSE pressure class.
  Body: actual linear Codazzi, mean-zero Poisson, finite trace/STF
  reconstruction and divergence. Support: ML50-ML52; AF/CG/GD bodies.

PL08 REAL_LINEAR_TWO_INVERSE_EQUIVALENCE
  Input: PL05-PL07 and original analytic germ/gauge semantics.
  Output: (PL.E1)-(PL.E3), real linearity, both inverse laws, and agreement
  with first variations of existing actual canonical families.
  Body: scalar/radial uniqueness, explicit pressure gauge and full-slice
  restriction. Support: ML22-ML25, ML34-ML39, ML49-ML54.

PL09 EXACT_QUADRATIC_AMBIENT_INTEGRATION
  Input: any actual linearized NSE pressure class, or its image under PL08.
  Output: exact forced family (PL.F1)-(PL.F2), actual normalized vacuum
  family, derivative identity and exact defect formula (PL.F3).
  Body: finite quadratic expansion, PL02, FB and linear normal uniqueness.
  Support: ML11-ML16, ML50-ML54; actual torus derivative/mean semantics.

PL10 EXISTING_FAMILY_PARAMETER_JET_HIERARCHY
  Input: an existing actual analytic forced family with fixed nu and
  canonical base, or the stated affine-viscosity variant.
  Output: (PL.F4)-(PL.F5) and the exact verified quadratic family jets.
  Body: finite binomial product differentiation and mixed-derivative
  commutation; no formal-series convergence inference.
  Support: ML07-ML12, ML50-ML54; ME21-ME22 only for actual diffusion symbols.

MATHEMATICAL_LINEARIZED_EQUIVALENCE = DERIVED_WITH_BOTH_INVERSES.
MATHEMATICAL_AMBIENT_INTEGRATION = ACTUAL_QUADRATIC_FORCED_FAMILY.
ARBITRARY_LINEARIZED_TO_CANONICAL_NONLINEAR_INTEGRATION = NOT_ASSERTED.

=======================================================================
D. PARAMETER-SEMANTIC AND EXTERNAL EVIDENCE AUDIT
=======================================================================

ML50-ML54 are exact differentiation interfaces for actual supplied maps.
They do not declare a differentiable structure on the entire set of
analytic PDE germs. Finite-dimensional family analyticity, a common
positive collar, coefficient differentiation, mixed-derivative equality,
and compatibility with quotient representatives are separate proved bodies.

An analytic solution of the linearized NSE equation is not, merely by
these source endpoints, the derivative of an exact unforced nonlinear NSE
family. Any use of family integration must supply an actual family and
its exact equations. An ambient forced family with stress of order two
may establish a first-order canonical variation without asserting a
nonlinear unforced integration theorem.

ME21-ME22 supply literal physical Fourier/Laplacian/viscosity semantics.
They do not supply scalar Hamiltonian CK completion, signed radial
geometry, analytic parameter gluing, or a forced-stress root theorem.
The source additions impose no new hypothesis on the original canonical
NSE-GR equivalence; they support the indexed new mathematical bodies.

Future external validation retains:
  Lean 4.33.0 + exact Mathlib/Methlib pins + compatible OS/architecture
  and dependency/source manifests + reused compatible caches;
  exact endpoint types + compiled actual bodies + exact-root axiom audit.
No sorryAx or unapproved axiom is permitted for A-tier promotion.
Prepared signatures and source-verified declarations are not A-tier evidence.

49. V1.5.0 PROOF PLACEMENT AND CACHED EXTERNAL VALIDATION SNAPSHOT
=======================================================================

49.1. Exact counts and roles.

  Parent contracts: NP01-NP16 = 16, unchanged.
  Existing atomic contracts: 99.
  New atomic contracts: FB01-FB10, VF01-VF10, PL01-PL10 = 30.
  Current atomic contracts: 129.
  Source declarations: ML01-ML54.
  Source endpoint groups: ME01-ME22.
  Section checkpoints: 32.

The new names are exact mathematical proof-body contracts, not inspected
Lean declarations. Every contract has its premises, conclusion and proof
in Sections 45-47. The original root remains the canonical zero-stress
restriction. The larger forced ambient theorem and linearized theorem
are additional derived statements, with their own explicitly stated
domains. No extra forced coordinate is added to the original NSE domain.

cylinder. Methlib's separate forward-time derivWithin convention is not
silently substituted. The matrix-inverse theorem requires a unit, and
continuous linear map theorems require the actual operator construction.
None of these source declarations supplies the new geometric equivalences.

49.2. Forced/boundary/vacuum factorization.

  actual analytic triple -> FB01 -> FB02 -> FB03;
  actual forced compatibility + FB03 -> FB04 -> constrained K;
  independent actual constrained K + GE + CK12 + GD -> FB05;
  actual Codazzi and nonlocal field identities -> FB06;
  complete normalized time-gauge identity -> FB07;
  FB03-FB04 + FB06-FB07 -> FB08;
  FB05 + FB08 -> FB09;
  actual vacuum Codazzi + the same completion identities -> FB10.

The intermediate boundary space is defined by conservation, Hamiltonian
and beta normalization before any completion map is used. Its vacuum
inverse uses the actual radial CK solution and propagated constraints.
The forced equivalence recovers the entire F, not only its divergence,
so no hidden stress quotient collapses the free tensor coordinates.

49.3. Complete ambient fibres and finite-jet boundary.

  actual analytic u,div u=0,partial_t<u>=0 -> VF01;
  VF01 + exact HD decomposition -> VF02;
  VF02 + FB09 -> VF03;
  zero-mode-aware GDelta identity + scalar/radial uniqueness -> VF04;
  VF03 + actual orthogonal projections -> VF05 -> VF06;
  actual fibre realization + HD norm identity -> VF07;
  actual source cancellation and div Z identity -> VF08 -> VF09;
  analytic initial u0,F0 + explicit compatible path -> VF10 initial image;
  actual time-polynomial free stress + radial finite-jet dependency FC07
    -> VF10 canonical/noncanonical equal-jet witnesses.

This proves compatible ambient realization at the full-germ level and
arbitrary analytic STF realization at the initial slice through the
explicit coordinate construction. It does not admit unrelated arbitrary
full-time u,F pairs without their forced compatibility equation.
The finite-jet separation is an actual vacuum result, not only a formal
tensor example. It limits jet-only admission tests, not all possible
finite certificates carrying additional checked analytic information.

49.4. Parametric and linearized proof composition.

  exact CK12 + unused parameter-jet projection -> PL01;
  PL01 + FB actual maps + compact gluing + analytic Fourier bounds -> PL02;
  actual base NSE + finite field calculus/Poisson -> PL03;
  actual coordinate inverse/connection/Ricci identities -> PL04;
  PL03 + exact Hamiltonian polarization + CK12 -> PL05;
  PL05 + trace reversal + linear radial CK + PL04 -> PL06;
  independent actual Glin equations + linear Codazzi -> PL07;
  PL05-PL07 + exact gauge/normal uniqueness -> PL08;
  explicit quadratic compatible forced family + PL02 + FB09 -> PL09;
  actual analytic family + finite binomial differentiation -> PL10.

PL08 is proved directly for the two independently defined linearized
solution spaces. Existence of a nonlinear canonical family is not a
premise. PL09 subsequently constructs an actual ambient vacuum family
with exactly quadratic constitutive defect for every such direction.
PL10 records the separate higher-order equations of any canonical family;
their existence is not inferred from first-order solvability.

49.5. The exact original root and the strengthened branches.

  Original root:
    N^omega_(nu,t0) ~= G^omega_(nu,rho,t0).

  Independent forced factorization:
    F^omega_(nu,t0) ~= K^omega_(rho,t0) ~= Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).

  Complete ambient chart, independent of viscosity:
    Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0)
      ~= U^omega_(t0) x M^omega_(t0) x Phi^omega_(t0) x TT^omega_(t0).

  Original canonical subset in these coordinates:
    f_nu(u)=0, M=0, phi=0, T=0.

  Exact linearized counterpart at any original canonical base:
    Nlin^omega_u ~=_R Glin^omega_g.

All spaces retain the specified analytic periodic representatives, local
quantifiers, fixed torus/time/normal marking and actual field equations.
Bulk spacetime dimension is five and Ric(g)=0 throughout the ambient
construction; the forced coordinate is a boundary constitutive tensor.
There is no bulk stress-energy source added to the Einstein equation.

The root can be assembled through FB09 and then restricted using exact
F recovery, or through the original GD10 chain. The two constructions
coincide by the same actual scalar/radial uniqueness and zero-stress
specialization, so this refinement does not define a different lift.

  Compiler/source alignment: external native Windows Lean 4.33.0.
  Mathlib: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d.
  Methlib: 09dfe94a1ef150e21d09df3013fbe09002ef139c.

    manifest and source hashes; preserve the pinned source alignment.
  A different Lean version requires its own compatible source/evidence tuple.
  Endpoint signatures -> independent opaque edge bodies -> safe native
    asynchronous elaboration -> deterministic final proof chain.
  Scheduling cannot change the theorem, premises, axioms or endpoint.

No Lean, Lake, compiler, build, cache retrieval or workflow was executed
for this revision. The repository interactions were read-only source
retrievals. No .lean artifact or run.cmd is created in B-tier mode.

A-tier promotion requires successful matching external compilation,
the exact theorem type and theorem-level axiom audit with no sorryAx
or unapproved axiom. Source verification, prepared contracts and a
mathematically derived checker interface are not that evidence.
Any later unresolved analytic, witness, certificate, admission, semantic
or checker premise must stay named in the exact formal theorem and its
closure classification. The present mathematical root acquires none.

=======================================================================
51. ROUND XVII: FINITE SPECTRAL CERTIFICATES FOR THE ACTUAL CONSTITUTIVE TENSOR
=======================================================================
MODE = B_TIER_DERIVATION
ROOT_SCOPE = UNCHANGED
LOCAL_LEAN_EXECUTION = NONE
ATOMIC_CONTRACTS = SF01-SF08

LEAN_CHECKPOINT CP51
  SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML01-ML06, ML26-ML28, ML41-ML42, ML48, ML55-ML58;
                     ME01-ME06, ME16-ME18, ME20, ME23-ME24.
  DEPENDENCIES = AF01-AF09; HD01-HD10; ST08; VF03,VF10.
  PARENT_REFINEMENT = NP02,NP11,NP13,NP14,NP15,NP16.
  ROLE = Finite residual observations with explicit analytic remainder;
         exact distinction between approximation, exclusion and admission.
  SOURCE_SCOPE = Scalar Fourier/Parseval and existing vector Leray support;
                 all tensor/weighted-tail certificate bodies below are new.
  LOOKUP = Section 54 for exact added pinned signatures and cutoff adapters.
  EVIDENCE = MATHEMATICAL_PROOFS; EXTERNAL_LEAN_NOT_ESTABLISHED.

XVII.0. Carrier, cutoff and norm conventions.

At a fixed real time, let F:T^3 -> STF_3(R) be the actual reconstructed
constitutive tensor, or any real STF tensor with ||F||_sigma <= B, sigma>0,
B>=0, using precisely AF.1. Write A_k=F_hat(k), w_k=exp(2*pi*sigma*|k|_1).
For N in N define the finite, negation-stable set

  Lambda_N={k in Z^3:|k|_1<=N},
  R_N F=sum_(k in Lambda_N) A_k exp(2*pi*i*k.x),
  Q_N F=F-R_N F,
  E_N(F)=sum_(k in Lambda_N)|A_k|_F^2,
  W_N(F)=sum_(k in Lambda_N)w_k|A_k|_F,
  b_N(F)=B-W_N(F)>=0,
  tau_N(F)^2=(1/2)exp(-4*pi*sigma*(N+1))*b_N(F)^2.   (SF.1)

The letter R_N in this section denotes Fourier truncation, not the
NSE differential residual R_nu. The cutoff retains k=0. Methlib's
mean-zero Galerkin cutoff cannot replace Lambda_N without an explicit
zero-mode adapter.

XVII.1. Exact finite observations and sharp real analytic tail.

Parseval and disjoint coefficient supports give

  ||F||_2^2=E_N(F)+||Q_N F||_2^2.                    (SF.2)

Every Pi_a from HD03 commutes with R_N and Q_N, since each is a Fourier
multiplier. Fourier truncation also commutes with P div and G div div on
the stated distributional domains. Thus the finite observations are
literal truncations of the actual physical tensor and its actual force.
They are not independent finite-dimensional model solutions.

Reality gives A_(-k)=conjugate(A_k). Choose one representative from each
pair {k,-k} outside Lambda_N and set a_k=|A_k|_F. No such k is zero. Then

  sum_(outside Lambda_N)|A_k|_F^2=2 sum_(representatives) a_k^2
      <=2(sum_(representatives)a_k)^2
      =(1/2)(sum_(outside Lambda_N)|A_k|_F)^2.

Absolute convergence follows from ||F||_sigma<infinity. Every omitted
frequency has the integer property |k|_1>=N+1, so

  sum_(outside Lambda_N)|A_k|_F
    <=exp(-2*pi*sigma*(N+1)) sum_(outside Lambda_N)w_k|A_k|_F
    <=exp(-2*pi*sigma*(N+1))*b_N(F).

Consequently

  0<=||Q_N F||_2^2<=tau_N(F)^2,
  E_N(F)<=||F||_2^2<=E_N(F)+tau_N(F)^2.              (SF.3)

The constant 1/2 in this real-tensor tail is sharp. Let D be any real STF
matrix with |D|_F=1 and let |k_*|_1=N+1. For

  F(x)=a cos(2*pi*k_*.x)D,

one has W_N=0, B=||F||_sigma=|a|exp(2*pi*sigma*(N+1)),
and ||Q_N F||_2^2=a^2/2=tau_N^2. Adding any real low-frequency tensor
retains sharpness when B is its exact weighted mass plus this pair's mass.
The factor 1/2 uses real-field conjugate symmetry; it is not asserted for
arbitrary complex-time values of a holomorphic continuation.

XVII.2. Certified force, pressure and four-component observations.

Define the computable finite energies

  D_N^2=(1/2)||R_N Pi_v F||_2^2,
  P_N^2=(2/3)||R_N Pi_s F||_2^2,
  T_N^2=||R_N Pi_T F||_2^2.

The zero mode is measured exactly as M=A_0. HD03 gives the exact identities

  E_N=|M|_F^2+2D_N^2+(3/2)P_N^2+T_N^2,
  d^2:=||P div F||_(dot H^-1)^2=(1/2)||Pi_v F||_2^2,
  p_F^2:=||G div div F||_2^2=(2/3)||Pi_s F||_2^2.

The tail projections are orthogonal and contractions. Therefore

  D_N^2 <= d^2 <= D_N^2+(1/2)tau_N^2,
  P_N^2 <= p_F^2 <= P_N^2+(2/3)tau_N^2,
  T_N^2 <= ||Pi_T F||_2^2 <= T_N^2+tau_N^2,         (SF.4)

and, more precisely,

  2(d^2-D_N^2)+(3/2)(p_F^2-P_N^2)
       +(||Pi_T F||_2^2-T_N^2)=||Q_N F||_2^2<=tau_N^2. (SF.5)

Thus the three errors share one tail budget. Independent upper bounds
in (SF.4) cannot all be treated as simultaneously attained. The lower
bounds for M, scalar and TT observations remain relevant to canonical
F=0 admission even when the projected-force observation D_N vanishes.

XVII.3. Finite approximate coefficient enclosures.

Suppose supplied finite coefficients Atilde_k and nonnegative errors eps_k
satisfy |A_k-Atilde_k|_F<=eps_k for every k in Lambda_N. Define

  l_k=max(0,|Atilde_k|_F-eps_k),
  u_k=|Atilde_k|_F+eps_k,
  W_low=sum_(k in Lambda_N)w_k*l_k,
  E_low=sum_(k in Lambda_N)l_k^2,
  E_high=sum_(k in Lambda_N)u_k^2,
  tau_cert^2=(1/2)exp(-4*pi*sigma*(N+1))*(B-W_low)^2.

The triangle and reverse-triangle inequalities give l_k<=|A_k|_F<=u_k.
Hence W_low<=W_N<=B, and the same real tail proof yields

  E_low<=||F||_2^2<=E_high+tau_cert^2.               (SF.6)

All interval arithmetic endpoints used in an implementation must enclose
the exact real expressions; an unvalidated floating-point value is not a
certificate. Neither the centers nor the errors are assumed to arise
from an exact Fourier solution. The actual F, its reality, and the valid
analytic norm bound remain explicit hypotheses of (SF.6).

If E_low>0 at a real time of an actual representative, its constitutive
tensor is nonzero there. On a connected representative cylinder this
excludes canonical germ admission: a real-analytic tensor identically
zero on a smaller cylinder would vanish throughout the connected common
cylinder by analytic identity. A comparison between unrelated representatives
or times outside the certified common domain is not permitted.

XVII.4. A finite time-frequency block with a uniform remainder.

Assume a holomorphic map F:D_R(t0)->W_sigma(STF_3(C)) with
sup_(|z-t0|<R)||F(z)||_sigma<=B, whose real restriction is real STF.
This is an explicit quantitative hypothesis obtained, with data-dependent
R,sigma,B, on a smaller common domain by AF01-AF09. Expand

  F(t)=sum_(j>=0) C_j(t-t0)^j,
  C_j=(partial_t^j F)(t0)/j!,
  ||C_j||_sigma<=B R^(-j).

The coefficient bound follows by the Banach-valued Cauchy integral on a
circle of radius r'<R and then by r' increasing to R. For integers J,N>=0
and real |t-t0|<=r<R put theta=r/R<1 and

  P_(N,J)(t)=sum_(j=0)^J R_N C_j (t-t0)^j.

This is a finite time-frequency polynomial. Decompose the exact error as

  F(t)-P_(N,J)(t)=Q_N F(t)+R_N sum_(j>J)C_j(t-t0)^j.

The two summands have disjoint Fourier supports. The first has real-field
L2 norm at most B exp(-2*pi*sigma*(N+1))/sqrt(2). The second has norm at
most B sum_(j>J)theta^j=B theta^(J+1)/(1-theta). Thus

  ||F(t)-P_(N,J)(t)||_2^2
    <=B^2[(1/2)exp(-4*pi*sigma*(N+1))
                  +theta^(2J+2)/(1-theta)^2]
    =:eta_(N,J,r)^2.                                (SF.7)

For r=0, theta^(J+1)=0. There is no 0^0 term. In particular

  max(0,||P_(N,J)(t)||_2-eta)<=||F(t)||_2
                             <=||P_(N,J)(t)||_2+eta. (SF.8)

The finite polynomial's L2 norm is computed by finite Parseval. (SF.7)
is a uniform real-time statement. Approximate time-frequency coefficients
require their separately certified finite synthesis error to be added.
No normal-family convergence or CK existence claim follows from this
certificate alone.

XVII.5. Relative energy driven by finitely observed geometric defects.

Let two actual reconstructed smooth flows v,V solve the tensor-forced
NSE equations on one existing compact common interval [s,t], and write
D=F-Fbar, y=||v-V||_2^2, L=||Sym grad V||_(L^infinity,op). Assume the
uniform analytic tensor estimate ||D(tau)||_sigma<=B on this interval,
with sigma>0. At each time form D_N(tau)^2 from D as in XVII.2 and
b_N(tau)=B-W_N(D(tau)). Define

  H_N(tau)=D_N(tau)^2
       +(1/4)exp(-4*pi*sigma*(N+1))*b_N(tau)^2.

Then d(tau)^2=||P div D(tau)||_(dot H^-1)^2<=H_N(tau).
Every displayed function is measurable and bounded on the common compact
interval under the analytic hypotheses. Substitution into ST08 gives

  y(t)<=exp(2 integral_s^t L)y(s)
       +(1/nu)integral_s^t exp(2 integral_tau^t L)H_N(tau)d tau. (SF.9)

If the velocity means agree, replace each exponent 2 integral_a^b L by
2 integral_a^b L-4*pi^2*nu*(b-a). The finite quantities can be further
bounded by supplied validated interval enclosures; the theorem itself
uses the displayed exact finite functions. A coarser valid certificate
replaces b_N(tau) by B. If the entire projected defect vanishes by an
independent exact argument, ST08 permits the stronger full-dissipation
rate; finite approximate vanishing alone does not justify that replacement.

This is an estimate for reconstructed velocities on the supplied interval.
It does not establish uniform radial stability or continuation of the
Einstein lift, and it does not create an existing NSE solution.

XVII.6. Infinite coefficient admission and limits of finite observations.

For an actual jointly analytic real constitutive germ, with C_j as above,

  F=0 as a germ
   iff forall j>=0, forall k in Z^3: (C_j)_hat(k)=0.  (SF.10)

Forward implication: differentiation and Fourier extraction preserve zero.
Reverse implication: Fourier reconstruction makes every C_j the zero
W_sigma element; its convergent Taylor series makes F zero on a common
smaller cylinder. The complex coefficient equalities may equivalently be
replaced by simultaneous real and imaginary scalar equalities. Both the
full spatial index and all time orders are required by this criterion.

For every fixed cutoff N and every sigma,B>0 there is an actual normalized
ambient metric g_bad with nonzero constitutive germ, yet

  R_N F_nu[K(g_bad)](t)=0 for all real t near t0,
  u(g_bad)=0,
  p(g_bad)=0 modulo the original pressure gauge,
  ||F_nu[K(g_bad)]||_sigma<=B.                       (SF.11)

Construction: choose k_*=(N+1,0,0) and
D_*=diag(0,1,-1)/sqrt(2). It is STF, |D_*|_F=1 and D_*k_*=0.
Choose any a with 0<|a|<=B exp(-2*pi*sigma*(N+1)) and set

  T(x)=a cos(2*pi*k_*.x)D_*.

Then T is real analytic, has zero mean and divergence, is independent
of time, and belongs to the TT range. VF03 supplies the actual ambient
vacuum metric Xi_(rho,t0)(0,0,0,T). Its exact reconstruction is u=0,
p=0, F=T. The canonical metric Xi_(rho,t0)(0,0,0,0) has precisely the
same retained residual modes, even including all time derivatives, and
the same velocity and pressure. The two marked metric germs are distinct
because their actual constitutive tensors are distinct. Only the residual
observations are asserted identical; their full low metric coefficients
are not asserted identical. This also realizes the sharp STF tail ratio
within actual ambient metric residuals.

Thus a fixed finite residual-mode gate, even augmented by the complete
reconstructed velocity/pressure and a positive analytic norm ceiling,
cannot decide canonical admission throughout the ambient sector.

A quantitative exclusion promise restores finite detection. If at some
certified real time ||F||_2>=delta>0, choose N with

  B exp(-2*pi*sigma*(N+1))<sqrt(2)*delta.

Then (SF.3) implies

  E_N(F)>=delta^2-(1/2)B^2exp(-4*pi*sigma*(N+1))>0.   (SF.12)

Such an N exists because sigma>0. This is a finite detection theorem
under a supplied nonzero lower gap, not a uniform gap for every nonzero
germ. For an arbitrary nonzero analytic germ, (SF.10) guarantees some
finite nonzero time-frequency coefficient; exact effective enumeration
or a decidable coefficient oracle is not inferred from analyticity.

XVII.7. Atomic contracts and source placement.

SF01 EXACT_RESIDUAL_FINITE_PROJECTION
  INPUT: Actual F in real STF L2, N in N.
  OUTPUT: Lambda_N finite and negation-stable; (SF.2); commutation with
          Pi_a, P div and G div div on their actual distributional domains.
  PROOF: XVII.0-XVII.1; coefficient supports and Parseval.
  SOURCE_SUPPORT: ML01-ML05,ML55-ML58; ME04-ME06,ME23; HD02-HD03.
  NEW_BODY: Finite integer-l1 cutoff with retained zero mode; componentwise
            real STF Parseval; commutation including distributional maps.

SF02 SHARP_REAL_ANALYTIC_TAIL
  INPUT: F real STF, sigma>0, ||F||_sigma<=B, N in N.
