=======================================================================
52. ROUND XVIII: RIGID COVARIANCE, GALILEAN RE-COMPLETION,
AND EXACT REMOVAL OF THE CONSTANT VELOCITY MODE
=======================================================================

STATUS = ! B_TIER; mathematical bodies below, target Lean applications uncompiled.
ROOT = NSE_GR_EXACT_SECTOR_EQUIVALENCE, unchanged.
PARAMETERS = nu>0, rho!=0, t0 in R; tau=t-t0.
ATOMIC_CONTRACTS = CV01-CV08.
LEAN_CHECKPOINT CP52
  REFINEMENT = CV01-CV08.
  SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML50-ML52, ML59; ME18-ME22; prior AF/FB/VF/GD bodies.
  EVIDENCE = source support only; target application uncompiled.
DEPENDENCIES = AF01-AF11, FB01-FB09, VF01-VF05, VF08, GD01-GD10.
SOURCE_SUPPORT = ML01-ML10, ML13-ML16, ML24-ML27, ML50-ML52;
                 ME01-ME10, ME18-ME22, with their recorded actual hypotheses.
ADDITIONAL_SOURCE = ML59: actual translation invariance of the torus integral;
  supply the additive Haar measure instances and integrability/type adapters.
NEW_BODIES = torus coordinate substitution, full tensor covariance, actual
            Galilean cancellation, marking obstruction, two product inverses.

XVIII.A. RIGID SPATIAL TORUS MAPS AND THE ACTUAL OPERATORS

Let A be a real 3x3 matrix with integer entries and A^T A=I. Every column
has integral squared entries summing to one, hence exactly one nonzero
entry, equal to +1 or -1. Orthogonality puts those entries in distinct rows.
Thus A is a signed permutation matrix. Conversely every signed permutation
satisfies the stated equations. In particular A^-1=A^T preserves Z^3, and
both |Ak|_1=|k|_1 and |Ak|_2=|k|_2 hold.

For b in T^3 define psi_(A,b)(x)=Ax+b. This is a well-defined analytic
measure-preserving torus diffeomorphism. Its inverse is psi_(A^T,-A^Tb).
Measure preservation follows directly by permutations, coordinate sign
reversal, and translations of periodic integrals over a period cube.
No general arbitrary real orthogonal matrix is admitted on the marked torus.

For scalars f, vectors v and symmetric tensors F put

  U0 f(t,x)=f(t,Ax+b),
  U1 v(t,x)=A^T v(t,Ax+b),
  U2 F(t,x)=A^T F(t,Ax+b)A.                         (SY.1)

The Fourier identities, with l=Ak, are

  hat(U0 f)(k)=exp(2*pi*i*(Ak).b) hat f(Ak),
  hat(U1 v)(k)=exp(2*pi*i*(Ak).b) A^T hat v(Ak),
  hat(U2 F)(k)=exp(2*pi*i*(Ak).b) A^T hat F(Ak)A.    (SY.2)

Proof. Expand the absolutely convergent analytic Fourier series. Its term
at l has frequency A^T l, so the coefficient at k comes from l=Ak.
The same proof holds in L2 by trigonometric-polynomial approximation and
unitarity. The phase is independent of the chosen lift of b, since Ak is
integral. Formula (SY.2) proves exact preservation of the W_sigma norms
of Section 27, and of Euclidean-vector/Frobenius-tensor L2 and isotropic Sobolev norms.

The constant matrix chain rule, A^T A=I, and (SY.2) give

  grad U0 f=U1 grad f,                 Delta Uj=Uj Delta,
  div U1 v=U0 div v,                   div U2 F=U1 div F,
  Z(U1 v)=U2 Z(v),                     Hess U0 f=U2 Hess f,
  TF(U2 F)=U2 TF(F),                   P U1=U1 P,
  G U0=U0 G,                          G=Delta_0^-1,
  <U0 f>=<f>, <U1 v>=A^T<v>, <U2 F>=A^T<F>A.       (SY.3)

For G and P, nonzero Fourier frequencies are permuted and the symbols
transform by orthogonal conjugation. At k=0, G_hat=0 and P_hat=I; these
are preserved exactly. Finite component derivatives prove the differential
identities first. Absolute differentiated synthesis proves their analytic
realizations on common smaller tubes. Therefore

  R_nu(U1 u,U0 p)=U1 R_nu(u,p),
  div U1 u=U0 div u.                                (SY.4)

The convection identity uses
(A^T u).(A^T grad) (A^T u)=A^T((u.grad)u), at the transformed point.
Thus the forced triple map (u,p,F)->(U1u,U0p,U2F) preserves its exact PDE,
STF condition, pressure-gauge relation, and canonical subset F=0.

XVIII.B. ACTUAL METRIC PULLBACK AND LIFT EQUIVARIANCE

Set D=diag(1,A), and define the collar map

  Psi_(A,b)(r,t,x)=(r,t,Ax+b).

For g=dr^2+h set g'=Psi_(A,b)^*g, so

  h'(r,t,x)=D^T h(r,t,Ax+b)D,
  K'(t,x)=D^T K(t,Ax+b)D.                           (SY.5)

It is still Gaussian, has the same chosen normal, and satisfies h'|r=0=eta
because D^T eta D=eta. The full collar Jacobian is E=diag(1,D). The actual coordinate Christoffel
formula and its constant-Jacobian chain rule give

  Ric(g')=Psi_(A,b)^* Ric(g).                        (SY.6)

In detail the inverse metric transforms by E^-1 on its two indices;
each differentiated metric contributes one constant E per lower index;
in the Christoffel expression each contracted pair cancels E E^-1.
Thus Gamma transforms by E^-1 on its upper index and E on both lower
indices. The derivative and quadratic terms defining Riemann have the
same transformation; contracting its upper/lower indices gives (SY.6).
This is a finite coordinate identity using GE01-GE06, with no separate
unproved general-diffeomorphism theorem.

The boundary definitions and (SY.3) then give

  beta'=U0 beta, q'=U0 q, v'=U1 v,
  p_*'=U0 p_*, F_nu[K']=U2 F_nu[K].                 (SY.7)

Consequently the normalized ambient vacuum sector and the canonical sector
are preserved. In particular the two normalization traces remain rho and 0.
Reconstruction commutes with these actual pullbacks.

For the scalar completion, if psi completes (u,p,F), then U0 psi has zero
Cauchy traces at the same t0 and

  S_F[U1u,U0p,U2F]+C[U0psi]

is the tensor transform of S_F[u,p,F]+C[psi]. Its Hamiltonian contraction
is therefore zero. Scalar CK uniqueness identifies U0psi with the actual
completion of the transformed triple. Equation (SY.5) and radial CK
uniqueness identify its vacuum lift with Psi_(A,b)^*g. Thus

  L_forced(U1u,U0p,U2F)=Psi_(A,b)^* L_forced(u,p,F),
  R_forced(Psi_(A,b)^*g)=(U1u,U0p,U2F).              (SY.8)

Both identities descend to germs by GD07-GD10. The same equations restrict
to the exact original canonical maps L and R. They are covariance identities
between different marked metric germs, not a quotient by these maps.
The pullbacks compose in their actual contravariant order; their inverses
use (A^T,-A^Tb).

The analogous time-translation identity is obtained from
Psi_s(r,t,x)=(r,t+s,x) and t0'=t0-s. All scalar zero data are imposed at
t0', and the entire two-sided cylinder is translated. Thus it is an
identity between the corresponding reference-time germ spaces; holding t0
fixed while translating arbitrary data is not the asserted theorem.

XVIII.C. GALILEAN COVARIANCE OF THE FORCED EQUATION

For c in R^3 let chi_c(t,x)=x-c(t-t0) in T^3, and define

  B_c(u,p,F)=(u_c,p_c,F_c),
  u_c(t,x)=u(t,chi_c(t,x))+c,
  p_c(t,x)=p(t,chi_c(t,x)),
  F_c(t,x)=F(t,chi_c(t,x)).                         (SY.9)

All fields are periodic and jointly real analytic. Explicitly, if the
original complex extension has time radius R and spatial tube width a,
choose R'<R and a'>0 with a'+||c||_infinity R'<a. Then the affine map
(tau,z)->(tau,z-c(tau-t0)) maps that smaller complex product into the old
one. Thus joint analyticity uses an actual positive smaller common domain.
No unchanged complex time radius is claimed as |c| grows.

Writing fields on the right at (t,chi_c(t,x)), the exact derivatives are

  partial_t u_c=partial_tu-(c.grad)u,
  (u_c.grad)u_c=(u.grad)u+(c.grad)u,
  Delta u_c=Delta u, grad p_c=grad p, div F_c=div F,
  div u_c=div u, Z(u_c)=Z(u).                       (SY.10)

Hence R_nu(u_c,p_c)=R_nu(u,p) composed with chi_c. In particular B_c
preserves the forced PDE and F=0. The time-pressure gauge transforms into
the same time-pressure gauge. Since chi_c composed with chi_d=chi_(c+d)
at fixed t,

  B_0=id, B_c B_d=B_(c+d), B_c^-1=B_(-c).            (SY.11)

The mean is <u_c>=<u>+c. The pressure mean is unchanged. For divergence-free
u the canonical pressure satisfies

  p0(u_c)=p0(u) composed with chi_c.                 (SY.12)

Indeed, double divergence of each mixed constant-velocity product is zero:
partial_i partial_j(u_i c_j+c_i u_j+c_i c_j)=0.
The inverse Laplacian commutes with spatial translations and removes the
same zero mode. The forced canonical pressure pi(u,F) transforms likewise.

XVIII.D. INTRINSIC METRIC ACTION BY UNIQUE RE-COMPLETION

Let Gtilde be the independent normalized vacuum space of Section 45.
Using the proved mutually inverse forced maps, define

  A_c(g):=L_forced(B_c(R_forced(g))).               (SY.13)

Every operation here is an existing explicit boundary reconstruction,
affine pullback of actual analytic fields, scalar CK completion, and radial
CK extension. The definition does not change the independent metric-sector
predicate. It is well defined on germ representatives and pressure gauge.
By (SY.11) and FB09's two inverse laws,

  A_0=id, A_c A_d=A_(c+d), A_c^-1=A_(-c),
  R_forced(A_cg)=B_c(R_forced(g)),
  F_nu[K(A_cg)]=F_nu[K(g)] composed with chi_c.       (SY.14)

In particular A_c restricts to an exact automorphism of the canonical
metric sector, and its canonical reconstruction is the ordinary Galilean
flow transformation. This action is independent of the viscosity used to
construct it on Gtilde. To prove independence, the viscosity transport
T_(nu,mu) of VF08 commutes with B_c, since Z(u_c)=Z(u) composed with chi_c.
The transported forced triple has the same metric lift by VF08, so the
nu and mu definitions in (SY.13) agree.

There is also a viscosity-independent expression in the complete chart
of Section 46. For g=Xi(u,M,phi,T),

  A_cg=Xi(u_c, M, phi composed with chi_c,
                    T composed with chi_c).        (SY.15)

The proof uses (SY.12), translation covariance of the fixed STF Fourier
projections, and exact recovery of all four chart coordinates. Here phi
is the scalar stress coordinate and T the mean-zero TT coordinate.
The function M(t) is unchanged. Formula (SY.15) holds as an identity of
marked metric germs, with any common smaller collar needed by completion.

XVIII.E. INITIAL BOUNDARY-JET FORMULA AND THE MARKING OBSTRUCTION

Let u0=u(t0), F0=F(t0), and put

  A0=((|u0|^2-rho)/3)I-u0 tensor u0+nu Z(u0)+F0,
  D_c(u0)=((2c.u0+|c|^2)/3)I
             -u0 tensor c-c tensor u0-c tensor c.    (SY.16)

Then tr D_c(u0)=0. Since chi_c(t0,x)=x, the transformed triple has
u0'=u0+c, F0'=F0, Z(u0')=Z(u0). Applying FB.C7 gives exactly

  K_0i[A_cg](t0)=K_0i[g](t0)+c_i,
  K_sp[A_cg](t0)=K_sp[g](t0)+D_c(u0),
  K_00[A_cg](t0)-K_00[g](t0)
    =[2A0:D_c(u0)+|D_c(u0)|_F^2
                           -4c.u0-2|c|^2]/(2rho). (SY.17)

This is a pointwise algebraic jet formula; it uses the full stress F0 and
is independent of the chosen pressure representative.

The re-completion action A_c is not asserted to be the coordinate pullback
by (r,t,x)->(r,t,x-c(t-t0)). For that coordinate map the boundary metric
would be

  -dt^2+|dx-c dt|^2
    =(-1+|c|^2)dt^2-2 sum_i c_i dt dx_i+|dx|^2,     (SY.18)

which equals eta only if c=0, as seen from the mixed coefficients.
Therefore for c!=0 this coordinate pullback is outside the ambient marked
space h|r=0=eta. Re-completion is essential. No Galilean diffeomorphism
covariance of the fixed-boundary Einstein sector has been assumed.

XVIII.F. EXACT CONSTANT-MEAN FACTORIZATION

For any forced solution triple, integrate its equation over T^3. Periodic
spatial divergence, pressure gradient and Laplacian all have zero mean;
for div u=0, convection is div(u tensor u). Hence

  partial_t<u>=0, m:=<u(t0)> in R^3.                (SY.19)

Let F^omega_0 be the subset of forced pressure-gauge germs with velocity
mean zero, and let N^omega_0 be its canonical F=0 subset. Set

  D0([u,p,F])=(m, B_(-m)[u,p,F]),
  I0(m,[w,P,H])=B_m[w,P,H].                         (SY.20)

The second coordinate of D0 has zero mean by (SY.11). Conversely I0 has
mean m. Equations B_m B_(-m)=id and B_(-m) B_m=id give both actual inverse
laws; no selection or integration theorem is used. Consequently

  F^omega_(nu,t0) ~= R^3 x F^omega_(nu,t0),0,
  N^omega_(nu,t0) ~= R^3 x N^omega_(nu,t0),0.        (SY.21)

Define Gtilde_0 and G_0 independently by adding

  <K_0bullet>(t0)=0                                (SY.22)

to the corresponding metric-sector predicates. Since reconstruction
preserves vector means, this equals zero reconstructed velocity mean.
For every g set m(g)=<K_0bullet>(t0). Then

  g |-> (m(g),A_(-m(g))g),
  (m,g0) |-> A_m g0                                (SY.23)

are mutually inverse maps, proving

  Gtilde_(rho,t0) ~= R^3 x Gtilde_(rho,t0),0,
  G_(nu,rho,t0) ~= R^3 x G_(nu,rho,t0),0.            (SY.24)

The original equivalence factors through these products and is the identity
on the R^3 factor. All membership predicates remain unchanged except for
the explicitly displayed definition of each zero-mean subspace.

XVIII.G. EXACT NORM TRANSPORT AND MEAN-ZERO SOURCE ADAPTER

At every real time, the k-th Fourier coefficient of a translated field
f composed with chi_c is exp(-2*pi*i*k.c*(t-t0)) hat f(k).
The phase has modulus one. Therefore for any admissible real s,

  ||u_c-(m+c)||_(dot H^s)=||u-m||_(dot H^s),
  ||u_c-(m+c)||_(H^s)=||u-m||_(H^s),
  ||F_c||_2=||F||_2, ||F_c||_sigma=||F||_sigma,
  ||u_c-(m+c)||_sigma=||u-m||_sigma.                (SY.25)

Each equality is asserted when that particular norm is finite. The W_sigma
identity uses the norm in AF.1. Gradients and STF Fourier components commute
with the translation, so their corresponding real-time spatial norms are
also preserved. The kinetic energy satisfies

  ||u_c||_2^2=||u-m||_2^2+|m+c|^2.                 (SY.26)

Proof: translation preserves the zero-mean fluctuation norm, and its cross
term with the constant m+c integrates to zero. No invariant common complex
time-strip norm is inferred from the real-time phase identity.

An external zero-mean periodic NSE endpoint can consequently be used at
arbitrary mean through the following exact mathematical adapter. Suppose
w is an actual periodic divergence-free solution on an interval I containing
t0, with mean zero, actual PDE R_nu(w,P)=0, and trace
w(t0)=u0-m where m=<u0>. Define

  u(t,x)=w(t,x-m(t-t0))+m,
  p(t,x)=P(t,x-m(t-t0)).                            (SY.27)

Then div u=0, R_nu(u,p)=0, u(t0)=u0, and <u>=m. The time interval is the
same. Joint C^k or C^infinity regularity is preserved by affine composition;
if and only if the supplied w is jointly analytic near the slice, the
construction produces the analytic input required by the present root.
The reverse uses c=-m. Uniqueness transfers in any solution class invariant
under these explicit affine maps, by applying the inverse transformation.
Norm-based mean-zero hypotheses transfer exactly by (SY.25).

This adapter discharges the constant-mode mismatch identified in Section
24.A0. It does not discharge the separate actual time-derivative semantics,
positive-time versus two-sided interval restriction, or analyticity mismatch
of a Methlib GlobalSmoothNSE endpoint. In particular mere C^infinity is not
silently promoted to joint analyticity. Source endpoints retain all their
own hypotheses, theorem types and later external axiom-audit obligations.

=======================================================================
53. ROUND XIX: EXACT POSITIVE-VISCOSITY IDENTIFICATION BY A GRAM MATRIX

LEAN_CHECKPOINT CP53
  SOURCE_ENDPOINTS = ML05, ML11-ML12, ML38-ML39, ML60-ML62; ME12, ME13, ME21-ME22.
  REQUIRED_BRIDGE_BODIES = VI01-VI08 below; NP12, NP13, NP15.
  PREVIOUS_BODIES = HD01-HD10, FB01-FB10, VF01-VF09, GD07-GD08.
  SOURCE_SCOPE = ME12 supplies actual spatial cancellations. ME13 assumes
    an actual unforced solution and positive-time global test predicates;
    it is not applied to an arbitrary ambient vacuum history here.
    The local forced energy identity is derived below from the actual
    reconstruction equation, with its own local-domain differentiation.
  LOOKUP = Sections 23-24, 48, 54-55; source evidence is not compilation.

FIXED DATA
  rho!=0; t0 in R; g in Gtilde^omega_(rho,t0).
  Choose one actual representative on a connected open time interval J
  containing [s,t], with s<t and s<t0<t. All spatial integrals have
  normalized measure on T^3. The metric collar is allowed to shrink.
  u=P K_(0 bullet), Z=Z(u), F0=TF(K_sp+u tensor u+Hess q).
  The symbol F0 is the algebraic residual at zero viscosity. It does not
  enlarge the root domain nu>0 or invoke a zero-viscosity existence theorem.
  Let H=L^2([s,t] x T^3; STF_3(R)), with Frobenius inner product.
  Set
    A=||Z||_H^2, B=<F0,Z>_H, C=||F0||_H^2,
    D=AC-B^2, E(tau)=||u(tau)||_2^2.
  The letters A,B,C,D in this section are real scalars, not the previously
  indexed stress operator A(f) or a spacetime tensor.

VI01 AFFINE_RESIDUAL_AND_ACTUAL_GERM_ZERO

For every real trial number lambda,
  F_lambda=F0-lambda Z,
and for lambda>0,
  g in G^omega_(lambda,rho,t0)
    iff ||F0-lambda Z||_H^2=0.                       (VI.1)

Proof. Since div u=0, tr Z=0, so trace-free projection commutes with
subtracting lambda Z. This proves the affine identity directly from
the independent K-only residual definition. The nonnegative squared
norm of a continuous tensor field has zero integral precisely when the
field is zero on the interior of [s,t] x T^3. This is a full time
neighborhood of t0, proving canonical germ admission. Conversely, germ
admission gives zero on some full time neighborhood of t0. At each fixed
x, each coefficient is real analytic on the connected J; analytic
uniqueness makes it zero on J. Thus its integral on [s,t] is zero.
This argument uses analytic identity continuation only within an already
existing connected representative. It does not construct a continuation.

VI02 QUADRATIC_OBJECTIVE_AND_GRAM_NONNEGATIVITY

For every lambda in R,
  J(lambda):=||F0-lambda Z||_H^2=C-2lambda B+lambda^2 A.
If A>0, put nu_*=B/A. Then
  J(lambda)=D/A+A(lambda-nu_*)^2,
  D=A||F0-nu_*Z||_H^2>=0.                           (VI.2)
If A=0, then Z=0 in H, B=0 and J(lambda)=C for all lambda.

Proof. Expand the real inner product. Completing the square gives
both identities for A>0, and positivity follows from the norm square.
For A=0, zero norm gives Z=0 in H and hence B=0. No equality-case
Cauchy--Schwarz theorem needs to be imported for this argument.
Equivalently the real symmetric matrix [[C,B],[B,A]] is positive
semidefinite: its quadratic form on (a,b) is ||a F0+b Z||_H^2.

VI03 EXACT_MINIMIZATION_OVER_POSITIVE_VISCOSITY

If A>0 and B>0, the unique minimizer of J on (0,infinity) is nu_*=B/A,
and min J=D/A. If A>0 and B<=0, then
  inf_(lambda>0) J(lambda)=C,
  forall lambda>0: J(lambda)>C,
so the infimum is not attained. If A=0, J is identically C.
For A>0, eta>=0, the exact sublevel identity is
  {lambda>0:J(lambda)<=eta^2}
    ={lambda>0: (lambda-B/A)^2<=(eta^2-D/A)/A}.       (VI.3)
In particular it is empty when eta^2<D/A.

Proof. Use (VI.2). If B<=0 and A>0, then A lambda^2-2B lambda>0
for every lambda>0, and this expression tends to zero as lambda tends
to zero from above. The sublevel formula is algebraic division by A>0.
Thus even an arbitrarily small positive-viscosity residual need not
attain its infimum at a permitted positive viscosity.

VI04 COMPLETE_CANONICAL_VISCOSITY_SET

Let V(g)={lambda>0:g in G^omega_(lambda,rho,t0)}. Then exactly one of
the following mutually exclusive alternatives holds:

  A>0, B>0, D=0:   V(g)={B/A};
  A=0, C=0:        V(g)=(0,infinity);
  otherwise:       V(g)=empty.                      (VI.4)

Proof. Combine VI01-VI03. For the degenerate case, A=0 and continuity
give Z(u)=0 on the interior time cylinder. The periodic strain identity
||Z(u)||_2^2=2||grad u||_2^2 gives grad u=0 there. Thus u(tau,x)=m(tau).
The ambient vacuum reconstruction has partial_tau<u>=0 by VF01, so m is
constant. C=0 then gives F0=0 and all positive viscosities are canonical.
The exact forced/bulk inverse identifies g with the common canonical
lift of this constant velocity. If A=0,C>0, none is canonical.

The set V(g), and the unique value B/A when V(g) is a singleton, is
independent of the admissible connected representative and interval.
This follows from VI01, not from invariance of A,B,C separately.
For a noncanonical metric, the least-squares value B/A may depend on
the interval. No interval-independent effective viscosity is asserted
outside canonical admission.

VI05 ENERGY_DROP_IDENTIFIES_THE_GRAM_CROSS_TERM

For every ambient normalized vacuum representative in this section,
  E'(tau)=-integral_(T^3) F0(tau):Z(tau),
  B=E(s)-E(t),
  A=2 integral_s^t ||grad u(tau)||_2^2 dr.              (VI.5)
Consequently, in the nonconstant canonical case,
  nu=(E(s)-E(t))/(2 integral_s^t ||grad u||_2^2 dr).
For arbitrary positive lambda,
  |E(s)-E(t)-lambda A| <= sqrt(A)*sqrt(J(lambda)).   (VI.6)

Proof. The actual reconstruction equation for any positive lambda is
  u_tau+div(u tensor u)+grad p_* -lambda Delta u=div(F0-lambda Z).
Since div Z=Delta u, cancellation gives
  u_tau+div(u tensor u)+grad p_*=div F0.
Multiply by u and integrate spatially. Periodicity and div u=0 cancel
the transport and pressure terms; integration by parts gives
  (1/2)E'=-integral F0:grad u=-(1/2)integral F0:Z.
Joint analyticity on a neighborhood of the compact time-space domain
justifies differentiating the integral. The ordinary fundamental theorem
of calculus and the strain identity prove (VI.5). Finally
  B-lambda A=<F0-lambda Z,Z>_H,
so the real Hilbert Cauchy--Schwarz inequality gives (VI.6), including
the A=0 case. This is an identity for arbitrary compatible ambient
histories; no unforced energy law was assumed in its derivation.

VI06 EXACT_OBSTRUCTION_SPLIT_AND_ENERGY_INSUFFICIENCY

Write the actual vacuum coordinates as g=Xi(u,M,phi,T), and set
  L=integral_s^t (|M|_F^2+(3/2)||phi||_2^2+||T||_2^2) dr,
  f0=u_tau+P div(u tensor u).
With the time-integrated mean-zero vector dot-H^-1 norm,
  C=2||f0||_(L^2_time dot-H^-1)^2+L,
  A=2||Delta u||_(L^2_time dot-H^-1)^2,
  B=2<f0,Delta u>_(L^2_time dot-H^-1).
If A>0, therefore
  D/A=2||f0-(B/A)Delta u||_(L^2_time dot-H^-1)^2+L. (VI.7)

Proof. VF04 and HD give F0=Aop(f0)+M+C_x(phi)+T, while
Z=Aop(Delta u). The four stress summands are orthogonal at each time,
and <Aop(f),Aop(h)>_2=2<f,h>_(dot H^-1) by polarization of HD03.
Integrate these identities in time and apply (VI.2). Each nonnegative
summand in (VI.7) must vanish for canonical admission. In particular,
the energy-balance scalar B=lambda A alone does not imply J(lambda)=0.

Actual counterexample to scalar sufficiency. Fix lambda0>0 and
  u(tau,x)=exp(-4pi^2 lambda0(tau-t0))*e1*cos(2pi x2),
  M=diag(1,-1,0), phi=T=0.
Then u is an actual jointly analytic NSE_lambda0 shear, and Xi produces
an actual normalized vacuum metric with F_lambda0=M. Since <Z>=0,
  A>0, B=lambda0 A, C=lambda0^2 A+(t-s)|M|_F^2,
  D=A(t-s)|M|_F^2>0.
Thus this metric satisfies the exact lambda0 energy balance but belongs
to no canonical positive-viscosity sector. Its recovered velocity does
solve unforced NSE; the obstruction is its independent metric stress.

VI07 QUANTITATIVE_STABILITY_WITH_NONDEGENERATE_STRAIN

Compare two actual pairs (F0,Z),(F0',Z') on the same fixed domain H.
Set A'=||Z'||^2, B'=<F0',Z'>, and suppose A>0,A'>0. If epsilon_F,epsilon_Z>=0 and
  ||F0'-F0||<=epsilon_F, ||Z'-Z||<=epsilon_Z,
then
  |A'-A|<=epsilon_Z(2sqrt(A)+epsilon_Z),
  |B'-B|<=epsilon_F sqrt(A)+sqrt(C) epsilon_Z
                         +epsilon_F epsilon_Z,
and
  |B'/A'-B/A|
    <=[epsilon_F sqrt(A)+sqrt(C)epsilon_Z+epsilon_F epsilon_Z
        +|B/A|epsilon_Z(2sqrt(A)+epsilon_Z)]/A'.      (VI.8)
An a priori bound A'>=a0>0 permits replacing the denominator by a0.

Proof. Expand Z'=Z+dZ and F0'=F0+dF. Apply Cauchy--Schwarz and the
triangle inequality to the two scalar differences. Finally use
  B'/A'-B/A=((B'-B)-(B/A)(A'-A))/A'.
This bounds the inferred scalar for supplied fields; it does not prove
continuity of the nonlinear radial Einstein construction or preserve
canonical membership under perturbation. The nonzero-strain lower
bound is essential; no uniform estimate through A=0 is claimed.

VI08 THREE_SCALAR_REDUCTION_AND_EVIDENCE_BOUNDARY

The independent metric-defined existential predicate has the exact form
  exists lambda>0: g in G^omega_(lambda,rho,t0)
    iff (A>0 and B>0 and AC=B^2) or (A=0 and C=0).   (VI.9)
It uses only reconstructed boundary fields and actual integrals, with
B replaceable by the observed energy drop. A proof of VI.9 composes
VI01,VI02,VI03,VI04 and introduces no extra analytic premise into the
original fixed-viscosity root. For a fixed prescribed nu>0, the exact
admission test remains C-2nu B+nu^2 A=0.

Three real integrals are a finite-dimensional characterization, not
automatically a terminating finite-data checker for exact equality.
Numerical intervals containing zero do not discharge J(nu)=0 or D=0.
The spectral bounds in Section 51 can certify strict positive defects
when their own checked analytic budgets and coefficient bounds are
supplied. Exact zero requires a separately proved identity or equivalent
sound certificate. No finite arithmetic encoding of arbitrary analytic
germs is inferred from (VI.9).

DEPENDENCY_ORDER
  VI01 -> VI02 -> VI03 -> VI04;
  VI01 + periodic cancellation/strain -> VI05;
  VI02 + HD03-HD10 + VF04 -> VI06;
  VI02 + Hilbert norm algebra -> VI07;
  VI04 + VI05 -> VI08.

STATUS = ! B_TIER, with all local/analytic/marked/vacuum premises retained.
LEAN_TARGET_BODIES = VI01-VI08; EXTERNAL_COMPILER_AND_AXIOM_AUDIT = ABSENT.

  FINITE_PHYSICAL_COMPONENT_PARSEVAL: 140-143.

  FINITE_PHYSICAL_REAL_ENSTROPHY_PARSEVAL: 193-196.
PLACEMENT = finite computed stress/velocity energies; finite strain Gram
  coefficients; derivative weighting with the retained unit-torus factors.
REMAINING_OBLIGATION = The cutoff is the NONZERO Euclidean integer ball,
  0<|k|<=N. In particular, finiteSynthesis has zero spatial mean, regardless
  of c(0). Restore the mean as a separate constant term if the desired
  cutoff contains zero. A cube or l1 cutoff is not definitionally this
  source cutoff. For stress use three row applications plus componentwise
  Frobenius summation and spectral reality for every row. These finite
  identities alone do not supply an infinite-field truncation/tail bound.
STATUS = SOURCE_VERIFIED_SUPPORT; TARGET_APPLICATION_UNCOMPILED

[ME24]
GROUP = NONNEGATIVE_WEIGHTED_CUTOFF_LERAY_CONTRACTION
IMPORT = SingularClosure.LerayL2Contraction
REVISION = 09dfe94a1ef150e21d09df3013fbe09002ef139c
BLOB_SHA = bd8772077bbc762e93919ca02097c05491604bf8
SOURCE = https://github.com/randogelist/Methlib/blob/09dfe94a1ef150e21d09df3013fbe09002ef139c/regularity-B-assembly/Singular-Axiomatic-closure/SingularClosure/LerayL2Contraction.lean#L90-L103
DIRECT_IMPORTS:
  SingularClosure.LerayPressure
  SingularClosure.GalerkinReality
  SingularClosure.FiniteParseval
SOURCE_CONTEXT_BEGIN
namespace SingularClosure
set_option autoImplicit false
lerayFourierCoeff c = fun k => coefficientLeray k (c k).
The finite summation convention is the same cutoffModes as in ME23.
SOURCE_CONTEXT_END
SOURCE_STATEMENT_BEGIN
theorem coefficientEnergy_leray_le (N : ℕ) (c : FourierCoeff) :
    coefficientEnergy N (lerayFourierCoeff c) ≤ coefficientEnergy N c

theorem coefficientWeightedEnergy_leray_le (N : ℕ) (c : FourierCoeff)
    (w : Frequency → ℝ) (hw : ∀ k, 0 ≤ w k) :
    (∑ k ∈ cutoffModes N, w k *
      (∑ i : Fin 3, Complex.normSq (lerayFourierCoeff c k i))) ≤
      ∑ k ∈ cutoffModes N, w k * (∑ i : Fin 3, Complex.normSq (c k i))
SOURCE_STATEMENT_END
SOURCE_LINE_MAP:
  coefficientEnergy_leray_le: 90-91.
  coefficientWeightedEnergy_leray_le: 97-102.
PLACEMENT = nonnegative spectral weights for finite projected-force bounds;
  H^{-1} mode weights 1/(4*pi^2*|k|^2) away from zero; weighted analytic
  and finite-cutoff L2 comparisons.
REMAINING_OBLIGATION = Establish the intended weight as nonnegative for ALL
  frequencies, including the separately specified zero value. Arbitrary
  Fourier coefficients are allowed algebraically; physical realization
  requires the additional reality condition and ME23. The sharp STF
  factor 1/2 is still the independently derived ST/HD matrix inequality;
  this Leray endpoint alone proves only projection contraction.
STATUS = SOURCE_VERIFIED_SUPPORT; TARGET_APPLICATION_UNCOMPILED

SF/CV/VI identifiers denote mathematical proof-body contracts defined in
Sections 51-53. They are not Lean declarations already present in Mathlib
or Methlib. ML59 has an explicitly documented source-generated additive
form; its evidence is SOURCE_VERIFIED_TO_ADDITIVE_SUPPORT. Every other
new source statement is literal pinned source, with carrier hypotheses
and target adapters recorded in Section 54. Neither category is a target
compiler result. The new branches use the already constructed actual
fields/metrics; none replaces the root's independent F_nu[K]=0 predicate.

Explicit finite-cutoff rule. Lambda_N in SF is an l1 ball including zero.
Methlib cutoffModes N is a nonzero Euclidean ball. Use ML55-ML58 directly
for the SF cutoff, or prove the exact index-conversion and zero-mode
adapter before using ME23-ME24. No definitionally equal cutoff is assumed.

  CV03-CV04 + conserved mean -> CV06;
  Fourier phase + Parseval -> CV07;

VISCOSITY:

  VI01 + real norm-square expansion -> VI02 -> VI03 -> VI04;
  VI01 + local energy/strain identities + FTC -> VI05;

  VI04-VI05 + explicit certificate scope -> VI08.

Each opaque target edge must have a complete proof body with its exact
premises. Native asynchronous elaboration may schedule independent edges
in the later external run. Pending signatures do not become proved merely
because the scheduler accepts them. The final composition is deterministic.

55.5. Root composition and branch boundaries.

The root's existing independent maps L,R and two inverse laws are unchanged.
CV06 provides an additional product decomposition of both existing carriers.
CV08 resolves only the mean-zero source mismatch; actual time analyticity,
source PDE semantics and the exact time domain remain mandatory.

SF02,SF04,SF05 quantify approximation under explicitly supplied analytic
budgets. SF08 proves the obstruction to a fixed finite residual-mode
acceptance gate on the full ambient sector. VI08 gives an exact three-real-
scalar characterization of existential viscosity, with no inference that
these scalars or their exact equality predicates are finitely computable.
Neither the quantitative hypotheses nor an unresolved admission certificate
is inserted as a new premise into the original root.

Canonical Galilean covariance is implemented by actual forced reconstruction
and unique re-completion. It is not the coordinate pullback that violates
h|r=0=eta. Rigid spatial isometries, by contrast, have the proved actual
metric pullback formula. No quotient by a new symmetry has been added to
the original marked metric carrier.
